// FILE: apps\busverse\src\app\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { AppHeader } from '@/components/layout/AppHeader';
import { SearchForm } from '@/features/search/components/SearchForm';
import { SearchResults } from '@/features/search/components/SearchResults';
import { SearchFilters, ScheduleSearchResult } from '@/features/search/types';
import { searchSchedules } from '@/features/search/api/search.api';
import { Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';

export default function HomePage() {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<SearchFilters>({
    originCity: null,
    destinationCity: null,
    date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
    sortBy: 'departureTime',
  });

  const [schedules, setSchedules] = useState<ScheduleSearchResult[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [hasSearched, setHasSearched] = useState(false);

  const handleSearch = async () => {
    if (!filters.originCity || !filters.destinationCity || !filters.date) return;

    setIsLoading(true);
    setHasSearched(true);
    try {
      const results = await searchSchedules({
        originCityId: filters.originCity.id,
        destinationCityId: filters.destinationCity.id,
        date: filters.date,
        sortBy: filters.sortBy,
      });
      setSchedules(results);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to search schedules:', err);
      setSchedules([]);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSortChange = (newSort: 'departureTime' | 'fare') => {
    setFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, sortBy: newSort }));
    setSchedules((prev) => {
      const copy = [...prev];
      if (newSort === 'fare') {
        return copy.sort((a, b) => {
          const fareA = typeof a.fare === 'object' ? a.fare.amountPaise : a.fare;
          const fareB = typeof b.fare === 'object' ? b.fare.amountPaise : b.fare;
          return fareA - fareB;
        });
      } else {
        return copy.sort((a, b) => new Date(a.departureTime).getTime() - new Date(b.departureTime).getTime());
      }
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas pb-20">
      {/* Retains Profile Badge and View Tickets Vault navigation */}
      <AppHeader />

      <main className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pt-8 md:pt-12">
        <div className="text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-8">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-brand-subtle border border-brand-border text-brand-primary text-xs font-bold mb-3">
            <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Next-Gen Transit Reservations</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-extrabold text-ink-bright tracking-tight leading-tight">
            Seamless Intercity Travel Across India
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-secondary mt-2">
            Real-time seat locks, authenticated Ed25519 boarding passes, and live GPS telemetry.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-10">
          <SearchForm
            filters={filters}
            onChange={setFilters}
            onSearch={handleSearch}
            isSearching={isLoading}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto">
          <SearchResults
            schedules={schedules}
            isLoading={isLoading}
            hasSearched={hasSearched}
            sortBy={filters.sortBy}
            onSortChange={handleSortChange}
          />
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\app\book\[scheduleId]\[lockId]\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth/AuthContext';
import { formatINR, formatISTTime } from '@/lib/utils/formatters';
import { fetchScheduleDetail } from '@/features/seats/api/seats.api';
import { createBooking } from '@/features/booking/api/booking.api';
import { PassengerManifestForm } from '@/features/booking/components/PassengerManifestForm';
import { PassengerInput } from '@/features/booking/types';
import { TransitLoader } from '@/components/brand/TransitLoader';
import { ThemeToggle } from '@/lib/theme/ThemeToggle';
import { BusVerseLogo } from '@/components/brand/BusVerseLogo';
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, AlertCircle, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

export default function BookingManifestPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ scheduleId: string; lockId: string }>;
}) {
  const resolvedParams = use(params);
  const { scheduleId, lockId } = resolvedParams;
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [schedule, setSchedule] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [boardingStandId, setBoardingStandId] = useState('');
  const [droppingStandId, setDroppingStandId] = useState('');
  const [passengers, setPassengers] = useState<PassengerInput[]>([]);
  const [seatLabels, setSeatLabels] = useState<string[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadData() {
      try {
        const sched = await fetchScheduleDetail(scheduleId);
        setSchedule(sched);

        // Pre-select first and last route stand
        const stands = sched.route?.stands || [];
        if (stands.length >= 2) {
          setBoardingStandId(stands[0].stand.id);
          setDroppingStandId(stands[stands.length - 1].stand.id);
        }

        // Recover selected seat list from query params or sessionStorage
        let storedLocks: Array<{ seatId: string; seatLabel: string; lockId: string }> = [];

        if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
          const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
          const seatsParam = urlParams.get('seats');
          const labelsParam = urlParams.get('labels');

          if (seatsParam && labelsParam) {
            const ids = seatsParam.split(',');
            const labels = decodeURIComponent(labelsParam).split(',');
            storedLocks = ids.map((id, i) => ({
              seatId: id,
              seatLabel: labels[i] || `Seat ${i + 1}`,
              lockId,
            }));
          } else {
            const saved = sessionStorage.getItem('busverse_checkout_locks');
            if (saved) {
              storedLocks = JSON.parse(saved);
            }
          }
        }

        if (storedLocks.length === 0) {
          storedLocks = [{ seatId: 'primary-seat', seatLabel: 'A1', lockId }];
        }

        setSeatLabels(storedLocks.map((l) => l.seatLabel));
        setPassengers(
          storedLocks.map((l, idx) => ({
            seatId: l.seatId,
            name: idx === 0 ? user?.name || '' : '',
            age: 25,
            gender: 'MALE' as const,
          }))
        );
      } catch (err: any) {
        setErrorMsg(err.message || 'Could not load itinerary.');
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }
    loadData();
  }, [scheduleId, lockId]);

  const handlePassengerChange = (index: number, updated: Partial<PassengerInput>) => {
    setPassengers((prev) => {
      const copy = [...prev];
      copy[index] = { ...copy[index], ...updated };
      return copy;
    });
  };

  const handleSubmitBooking = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!boardingStandId || !droppingStandId) {
      setErrorMsg('Please select distinct boarding and dropping stands.');
      return;
    }

    if (boardingStandId === droppingStandId) {
      setErrorMsg('Boarding and dropping stands must differ.');
      return;
    }

    // Verify all passenger names are filled
    const missingNameIndex = passengers.findIndex((p) => !p.name.trim());
    if (missingNameIndex !== -1) {
      setErrorMsg(`Please enter the legal name for Passenger ${missingNameIndex + 1}.`);
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    try {
      const booking = await createBooking({
        lockId,
        boardingStandId,
        droppingStandId,
        passengers: passengers.map((p) => ({
          seatId: p.seatId,
          name: p.name.trim(),
          age: Number(p.age) || 25,
          gender: p.gender || 'MALE',
        })),
      });

      router.push(`/pay/${booking.bookingId}`);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message || 'Reservation could not be initialized. Your seat lock may have expired.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas flex items-center justify-center">
        <TransitLoader caption="PREPARING MANIFEST" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const standsList =
    schedule?.route?.stands?.map((s: any) => ({
      id: s.stand.id,
      name: s.stand.name,
      type: s.stand.type,
      sequence: s.sequence,
    })) || [];

  const farePaise =
    typeof schedule?.fare === 'object' ? schedule.fare.amountPaise : schedule?.fare || 0;
  const totalAmountPaise = farePaise * passengers.length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas pb-28">
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-surface-card/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-border-hairline shadow-sm">
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 h-16 flex items-center justify-between">
          <Link
            href={`/schedule/${scheduleId}`}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-ink-secondary hover:text-brand-primary transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Change Seats</span>
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <BusVerseLogo size={32} variant="mark" />
            <ThemeToggle />
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pt-6">
        {schedule && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <div className="text-base font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
                {schedule.route.originCity.name} → {schedule.route.destinationCity.name}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-ink-secondary mt-0.5">
                {schedule.operatorName} • Departs {formatISTTime(schedule.departureTime)}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-ink-muted block">
                Total ({passengers.length} {passengers.length === 1 ? 'Seat' : 'Seats'})
              </span>
              <span className="text-xl font-extrabold text-ink-bright">{formatINR(totalAmountPaise)}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {errorMsg && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-xl bg-danger-subtle border border-danger-crimson text-danger-crimson text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span>{errorMsg}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmitBooking}>
          <PassengerManifestForm
            stands={standsList}
            boardingStandId={boardingStandId}
            droppingStandId={droppingStandId}
            onBoardingChange={setBoardingStandId}
            onDroppingChange={setDroppingStandId}
            passengers={passengers}
            seatLabels={seatLabels}
            onPassengerChange={handlePassengerChange}
          />

          <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40 bg-surface-card/95 backdrop-blur-md border-t border-border-strong shadow-modal p-4">
            <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <div className="text-xs text-ink-muted">Confirmed Hold ({passengers.length} seats)</div>
                <div className="text-lg font-extrabold text-ink-bright">{formatINR(totalAmountPaise)}</div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={submitting}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl bg-brand-primary text-white hover:bg-brand-hover shadow-glow disabled:opacity-50 transition-all cursor-pointer"
              >
                {submitting ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                    <span>Reserving Booking...</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <span>Proceed to Payment</span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </form>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\app\bookings\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { fetchPassengerBookings } from '@/features/tickets/api/tickets.api';
import { BookingResponse } from '@/features/booking/types';
import { formatINR, formatISTTime, formatISTDate } from '@/lib/utils/formatters';
import { TransitLoader } from '@/components/brand/TransitLoader';
import { ThemeToggle } from '@/lib/theme/ThemeToggle';
import { BusVerseLogo } from '@/components/brand/BusVerseLogo';
import { ArrowLeft, Ticket, ArrowRight, ShieldCheck, Bus } from 'lucide-react';

export default function MyTripsPage() {
  const [bookings, setBookings] = useState<BookingResponse[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function load() {
      try {
        const res = await fetchPassengerBookings(20);
        setBookings(res.data);
      } catch (e) {
        setBookings([]);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }
    load();
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas pb-20">
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-surface-card/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-border-hairline shadow-sm">
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 h-16 flex items-center justify-between">
          <Link
            href="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-ink-secondary hover:text-brand-primary transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Search Home</span>
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <BusVerseLogo size={32} variant="mark" />
            <ThemeToggle />
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pt-8">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-ink-bright tracking-tight mb-2">My Bookings</h1>
        <p className="text-xs text-ink-secondary mb-6">Manage confirmed boarding passes and review past journeys</p>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="py-12 flex justify-center">
            <TransitLoader caption="LOCATING TRIPS" />
          </div>
        ) : bookings.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-12 text-center rounded-3xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient">
            <Ticket className="w-10 h-10 text-ink-muted mx-auto mb-3" />
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-ink-bright">No Bookings Yet</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-secondary mt-1 mb-6">
              Search for coaches across verified intercity routes to reserve your first journey.
            </p>
            <Link
              href="/"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl bg-brand-primary text-white hover:bg-brand-hover shadow-glow"
            >
              <span>Explore Routes</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {bookings.map((b) => (
              <Link
                key={b.bookingId}
                href={`/bookings/${b.bookingId}`}
                className="block p-5 rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline hover:border-brand-primary shadow-ambient transition-all duration-200"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-border-hairline">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Bus className="w-4 h-4 text-brand-primary" />
                    <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-ink-bright">
                      {b.bookingReference}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <span
                    className={`text-[10px] font-mono font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border ${
                      b.status === 'CONFIRMED'
                        ? 'bg-signal-subtle border-signal-emerald text-signal-emerald'
                        : b.status === 'CANCELLED'
                        ? 'bg-danger-subtle border-danger-crimson text-danger-crimson'
                        : 'bg-surface-sunken text-ink-muted'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {b.status}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
                      {b.boardingStand?.name || 'Origin'} → {b.droppingStand?.name || 'Destination'}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[11px] text-ink-muted mt-0.5">
                      {b.createdAt ? formatISTDate(b.createdAt) : 'Confirmed Pass'}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="text-right">
                    <div className="font-extrabold text-sm text-ink-bright">
                      {formatINR(b.totalFarePaise || 0)}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-brand-primary font-bold flex items-center gap-1 justify-end mt-0.5">
                      <span>View Pass</span>
                      <ArrowRight className="w-3 h-3" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\app\bookings\[id]\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useWsEvent } from '@/lib/ws/useWsEvent';
import { fetchBookingDetail } from '@/features/booking/api/booking.api';
import { fetchBookingTicket, fetchRefundPreview } from '@/features/tickets/api/tickets.api';
import { BookingResponse } from '@/features/booking/types';
import { TicketData, RefundPreviewData } from '@/features/tickets/types';
import { TicketStub } from '@/features/tickets/components/TicketStub';
import { CancelModal } from '@/features/tickets/components/CancelModal';
import { TransitLoader } from '@/components/brand/TransitLoader';
import { ThemeToggle } from '@/lib/theme/ThemeToggle';
import { BusVerseLogo } from '@/components/brand/BusVerseLogo';
import { ArrowLeft, Radio, Trash2, AlertCircle, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';

export default function TicketDetailPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
}) {
  const resolvedParams = use(params);
  const bookingId = resolvedParams.id;
  const router = useRouter();

  const [booking, setBooking] = useState<BookingResponse | null>(null);
  const [ticket, setTicket] = useState<TicketData | null>(null);
  const [refundPreview, setRefundPreview] = useState<RefundPreviewData | null>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isCancelModalOpen, setIsCancelModalOpen] = useState(false);

  const loadTicketAndBooking = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    try {
      const bookingData = await fetchBookingDetail(bookingId);
      setBooking(bookingData);

      const ticketData = await fetchBookingTicket(bookingId);
      setTicket(ticketData);

      // Pre-fetch cancellation refund preview if booking is confirmed
      if (bookingData.status === 'CONFIRMED') {
        const preview = await fetchRefundPreview(bookingId).catch(() => null);
        setRefundPreview(preview);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message || 'Unable to retrieve ticket pass.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTicketAndBooking();
  }, [bookingId]);

  // Real-time: update ticket status if driver scans QR
  useWsEvent(`booking:${bookingId}`, 'boarding:verified', () => {
    setTicket((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, status: 'BOARDED' } : null));
  });

  useWsEvent(`booking:${bookingId}`, 'booking:cancelled', () => {
    setBooking((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, status: 'CANCELLED' } : null));
  });

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas flex items-center justify-center">
        <TransitLoader caption="VERIFYING CRYPTOGRAPHIC PASS" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const isCancellable = booking?.status === 'CONFIRMED' && ticket?.status === 'ACTIVE';

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas pb-20">
      {/* Top Header */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-surface-card/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-border-hairline shadow-sm">
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 h-16 flex items-center justify-between">
          <Link
            href="/bookings"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-ink-secondary hover:text-brand-primary transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>My Bookings</span>
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <BusVerseLogo size={32} variant="mark" />
            <ThemeToggle />
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="max-w-2xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pt-6">
        {errorMsg && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-xl bg-danger-subtle border border-danger-crimson text-danger-crimson text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{errorMsg}</span>
            </div>
            <button onClick={loadTicketAndBooking} className="flex items-center gap-1 underline text-xs">
              <RefreshCw className="w-3 h-3" /> Retry
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Boarding Pass Stub */}
        {booking && ticket && (
          <div className="mb-8">
            <TicketStub booking={booking} ticket={ticket} />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Action Tray */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-3">
          {/* Live Tracking CTA */}
          <Link
            href={`/trips/${booking?.scheduleId || 'trip-demo'}/live`}
            className="w-full sm:flex-1 py-3 px-4 rounded-xl bg-brand-primary text-white hover:bg-brand-hover shadow-glow flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold transition-all"
          >
            <Radio className="w-4 h-4 animate-pulse text-telemetry-cyan" />
            <span>Track Bus Live on Map</span>
          </Link>

          {/* Cancellation Trigger */}
          {isCancellable && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsCancelModalOpen(true)}
              className="w-full sm:w-auto py-3 px-4 rounded-xl border border-border-strong bg-surface-card hover:bg-danger-subtle hover:border-danger-crimson text-ink-secondary hover:text-danger-crimson flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Cancel Booking</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Refund Cancellation Dialog */}
        <CancelModal
          isOpen={isCancelModalOpen}
          onClose={() => setIsCancelModalOpen(false)}
          bookingId={bookingId}
          preview={refundPreview}
          onCancelled={() => {
            loadTicketAndBooking();
          }}
        />
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\app\pay\[bookingId]\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useWsEvent } from '@/lib/ws/useWsEvent';
import { formatINR, formatISTTime } from '@/lib/utils/formatters';
import { fetchBookingDetail } from '@/features/booking/api/booking.api';
import { BookingResponse } from '@/features/booking/types';
import { BookingExpiryTimer } from '@/features/booking/components/BookingExpiryTimer';
import { initiatePayment, devMockConfirmPayment } from '@/features/payments/api/payments.api';
import { PaymentMethod } from '@/features/payments/types';
import { TransitLoader } from '@/components/brand/TransitLoader';
import { ThemeToggle } from '@/lib/theme/ThemeToggle';
import { BusVerseLogo } from '@/components/brand/BusVerseLogo';
import { CONFIG } from '@/lib/config';
import {
  CreditCard,
  QrCode,
  Building2,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
  Loader2,
  ArrowRight,
  ShieldAlert,
  Zap,
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function PaymentCheckoutPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ bookingId: string }>;
}) {
  const resolvedParams = use(params);
  const { bookingId } = resolvedParams;
  const router = useRouter();

  const [booking, setBooking] = useState<BookingResponse | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [selectedMethod, setSelectedMethod] = useState<PaymentMethod>('UPI');
  const [paymentInitiating, setPaymentInitiating] = useState(false);
  const [activePaymentId, setActivePaymentId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [paymentConfirmed, setPaymentConfirmed] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Load authoritative booking status and expiresAt
  const loadBooking = async () => {
    try {
      const data = await fetchBookingDetail(bookingId);
      setBooking(data);
      if (data.status === 'CONFIRMED') {
        setPaymentConfirmed(true);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message || 'Unable to retrieve reservation details.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadBooking();
  }, [bookingId]);

  // Real-time WebSocket: Listen for instant confirmation signals
  useWsEvent(`booking:${bookingId}`, 'payment:succeeded', () => {
    setPaymentConfirmed(true);
  });

  useWsEvent(`booking:${bookingId}`, 'booking:confirmed', () => {
    setPaymentConfirmed(true);
  });

  useWsEvent(`booking:${bookingId}`, 'booking:expired', () => {
    setErrorMsg('The 15-minute booking window elapsed. This reservation has expired.');
  });

  // Handle payment checkout initiation
  const handleInitiatePayment = async () => {
    setPaymentInitiating(true);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    try {
      const res = await initiatePayment({
        bookingId,
        method: selectedMethod,
      });

      setActivePaymentId(res.paymentId);

      // In development: prompt instant webhook bypass
      // In production: open Razorpay checkout modal or window.location.href = res.checkoutUrl
      if (res.checkoutUrl && !CONFIG.IS_DEV) {
        window.location.href = res.checkoutUrl;
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message || 'Failed to initialize payment gateway.');
    } finally {
      setPaymentInitiating(false);
    }
  };

  // Dev shortcut: Simulate Razorpay payment.captured webhook
  const handleSimulateWebhook = async () => {
    if (!activePaymentId) return;
    setPaymentInitiating(true);
    try {
      await devMockConfirmPayment(activePaymentId);
      setPaymentConfirmed(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message || 'Failed to execute simulated capture.');
    } finally {
      setPaymentInitiating(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas flex items-center justify-center">
        <TransitLoader caption="CONFIGURING GATEWAY" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (paymentConfirmed) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas flex items-center justify-center p-6">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md p-8 rounded-3xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-floating text-center">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 mx-auto rounded-full bg-signal-subtle text-signal-emerald flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-10 h-10 animate-bounce" />
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-ink-bright">Payment Confirmed!</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-ink-secondary mt-1">
            Reference: <span className="font-mono font-bold text-ink-bright">{booking?.bookingReference}</span>
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-signal-emerald font-semibold mt-2">
            Your Ed25519 cryptographic boarding pass has been issued.
          </p>

          <button
            onClick={() => router.push(`/bookings/${bookingId}`)}
            className="mt-6 w-full py-3 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl bg-brand-primary text-white hover:bg-brand-hover shadow-glow flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <span>View Boarding Ticket</span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas pb-20">
      {/* Top Header */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-surface-card/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-border-hairline shadow-sm">
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 h-16 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <BusVerseLogo size={32} variant="mark" />
            <span className="text-base font-extrabold text-ink-bright">Secure Checkout</span>
          </div>
          <ThemeToggle />
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="max-w-2xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pt-6">
        {/* Authoritative 15-Min Expiry Banner */}
        {booking?.expiresAt && (
          <div className="mb-6 flex justify-center">
            <BookingExpiryTimer
              expiresAt={booking.expiresAt}
              onExpire={() => {
                setErrorMsg('Your 15-minute reservation expired.');
              }}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {errorMsg && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-xl bg-danger-subtle border border-danger-crimson text-danger-crimson text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span>{errorMsg}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Fare Summary Card */}
        <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-4 border-b border-border-hairline">
            <div>
              <span className="text-[11px] font-mono uppercase text-ink-muted block">Booking Reference</span>
              <span className="text-sm font-mono font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
                {booking?.bookingReference || 'PENDING'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-mono uppercase text-ink-muted block">Amount Due</span>
              <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
                {formatINR(booking?.totalFarePaise || 0)}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="pt-4 space-y-2 text-xs">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-ink-secondary">
              <span>Seats Allocated:</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-ink-bright">
                {booking?.passengers?.map((p) => p.seatLabel).join(', ') || 'Assigned'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-ink-secondary">
              <span>Pickup Point:</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-ink-bright">{booking?.boardingStand?.name || 'Main Stand'}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-ink-secondary">
              <span>Drop Point:</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-ink-bright">{booking?.droppingStand?.name || 'Terminal'}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Payment Methods Card */}
        <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-bright mb-4">
            Select Payment Method
          </h2>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {/* UPI Option */}
            <label
              className={`flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-xl border-2 cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                selectedMethod === 'UPI'
                  ? 'border-brand-primary bg-brand-subtle'
                  : 'border-border-subtle bg-canvas hover:bg-surface-hover'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <input
                  type="radio"
                  name="paymentMethod"
                  value="UPI"
                  checked={selectedMethod === 'UPI'}
                  onChange={() => setSelectedMethod('UPI')}
                  className="hidden"
                />
                <QrCode className="w-5 h-5 text-brand-primary" />
                <div>
                  <div className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-bright">UPI Instant Pay</div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-ink-secondary">GPay, Paytm, PhonePe, BHIM</div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-signal-subtle text-signal-emerald">
                Fastest
              </span>
            </label>

            {/* Card Option */}
            <label
              className={`flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-xl border-2 cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                selectedMethod === 'CARD'
                  ? 'border-brand-primary bg-brand-subtle'
                  : 'border-border-subtle bg-canvas hover:bg-surface-hover'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <input
                  type="radio"
                  name="paymentMethod"
                  value="CARD"
                  checked={selectedMethod === 'CARD'}
                  onChange={() => setSelectedMethod('CARD')}
                  className="hidden"
                />
                <CreditCard className="w-5 h-5 text-brand-primary" />
                <div>
                  <div className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-bright">Credit / Debit Card</div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-ink-secondary">Visa, Mastercard, RuPay</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </label>

            {/* Netbanking Option */}
            <label
              className={`flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-xl border-2 cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                selectedMethod === 'NETBANKING'
                  ? 'border-brand-primary bg-brand-subtle'
                  : 'border-border-subtle bg-canvas hover:bg-surface-hover'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <input
                  type="radio"
                  name="paymentMethod"
                  value="NETBANKING"
                  checked={selectedMethod === 'NETBANKING'}
                  onChange={() => setSelectedMethod('NETBANKING')}
                  className="hidden"
                />
                <Building2 className="w-5 h-5 text-brand-primary" />
                <div>
                  <div className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-bright">Net Banking</div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-ink-secondary">All major Indian banks</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </label>
          </div>

          {/* Action Trigger */}
          <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
            {!activePaymentId ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleInitiatePayment}
                disabled={paymentInitiating}
                className="w-full py-3.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl bg-brand-primary text-white hover:bg-brand-hover shadow-glow disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer transition-all"
              >
                {paymentInitiating ? (
                  <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <span>Pay {formatINR(booking?.totalFarePaise || 0)}</span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            ) : CONFIG.IS_DEV ? (
              /* Development Fast-Track Button */
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleSimulateWebhook}
                disabled={paymentInitiating}
                className="w-full py-3.5 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-xl bg-signal-emerald text-white hover:opacity-90 shadow-glow flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {paymentInitiating ? (
                  <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Zap className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>[DEV] Simulate Razorpay Capture Webhook</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\app\profile\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth/AuthContext';
import { fetchMyBookings } from '@/features/booking/api/booking.api';
import { AppHeader } from '@/components/layout/AppHeader';
import { TransitLoader } from '@/components/brand/TransitLoader';
import { formatINR, formatISTTime } from '@/lib/utils/formatters';
import {
  User,
  Phone,
  ShieldCheck,
  LogOut,
  Ticket,
  ArrowRight,
  Clock,
  MapPin,
  AlertCircle,
  Armchair,
  RefreshCw,
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function ProfilePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, isAuthenticated, logout } = useAuth();

  const [bookings, setBookings] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const loadBookings = async () => {
    setErrorMsg(null);
    try {
      const data = await fetchMyBookings();
      setBookings(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message || 'Failed to load booking history.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAuthenticated && !loading) {
      router.push('/');
      return;
    }
    loadBookings();
  }, [isAuthenticated]);

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    router.push('/');
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas flex items-center justify-center">
        <TransitLoader caption="LOADING YOUR TICKET VAULT" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas pb-24">
      <AppHeader backHref="/" backLabel="All Schedules" />

      <main className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pt-6 space-y-6">
        {/* Profile Card */}
        <section className="p-6 rounded-3xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-brand-subtle border-2 border-brand-border flex items-center justify-center text-brand-primary">
                <User className="w-7 h-7" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <h1 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
                    {user?.name || 'Verified Passenger'}
                  </h1>
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono font-bold bg-signal-subtle text-signal-emerald border border-signal-emerald/30">
                    <ShieldCheck className="w-3 h-3" />
                    Verified
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-ink-muted mt-1 font-mono">
                  <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>{user?.phone || 'E.164 Authenticated'}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={handleLogout}
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl bg-canvas border border-border-strong text-ink-secondary hover:text-danger-crimson hover:border-danger-crimson/40 transition-colors cursor-pointer self-start sm:self-auto"
            >
              <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Sign Out</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* Tickets Section */}
        <section className="space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Ticket className="w-4 h-4 text-brand-primary" />
              <h2 className="text-sm font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-bright">
                Your Tickets & Reservations ({bookings.length})
              </h2>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setLoading(true);
                loadBookings();
              }}
              className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-ink-muted hover:text-ink-bright hover:bg-surface-elevated transition-colors text-xs flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">Refresh</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {errorMsg && (
            <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-danger-subtle border border-danger-crimson text-danger-crimson text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{errorMsg}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {bookings.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-12 text-center rounded-3xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient">
              <Ticket className="w-12 h-12 text-ink-muted mx-auto mb-3 opacity-40" />
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-ink-bright">No Bookings Found</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-secondary mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                You haven&apos;t reserved any journeys yet. Browse available intercity routes to book a seat.
              </p>
              <Link
                href="/"
                className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-brand-primary text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-brand-hover shadow-glow transition-all"
              >
                <span>Find Bus Schedules</span>
                <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {bookings.map((booking) => {
                const bId = booking.id || booking.bookingId;
                const ref = booking.bookingReference || booking.reference || bId.slice(0, 8);
                const isConfirmed = booking.status === 'CONFIRMED' || booking.status === 'COMPLETED';
                const isPending = booking.status === 'PENDING';

                const routeOrigin =
                  booking.schedule?.route?.originCity?.name ||
                  booking.originCity ||
                  booking.boardingStand?.name ||
                  'Departure';
                const routeDestination =
                  booking.schedule?.route?.destinationCity?.name ||
                  booking.destinationCity ||
                  booking.droppingStand?.name ||
                  'Arrival';

                const depTime = booking.schedule?.departureTime || booking.departureTime;
                const passengers = booking.passengers || [];
                const seatLabels = passengers
                  .map((p: any) => p.seatLabel || p.seat?.label)
                  .filter(Boolean);

                const fareAmount =
                  booking.totalFareAmount || booking.totalFarePaise || booking.totalAmountPaise || 0;

                return (
                  <div
                    key={bId}
                    className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient hover:border-brand-border/60 transition-all"
                  >
                    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-border-hairline gap-2">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-xs font-mono font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
                          {ref}
                        </span>
                        <span
                          className={`text-[10px] font-mono font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                            isConfirmed
                              ? 'bg-signal-subtle text-signal-emerald border border-signal-emerald/30'
                              : isPending
                              ? 'bg-warning-subtle text-amber-500 border border-amber-500/30'
                              : 'bg-canvas text-ink-muted border border-border-strong'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {booking.status}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="text-left sm:text-right">
                        <span className="text-base font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
                          {formatINR(fareAmount)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-12 gap-4 py-4 text-xs items-center">
                      <div className="sm:col-span-6">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
                          <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-brand-primary flex-shrink-0" />
                          <span>{routeOrigin} → {routeDestination}</span>
                        </div>
                        {depTime && (
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-ink-secondary mt-1">
                            <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-ink-muted" />
                            <span>Departure: {formatISTTime(depTime)}</span>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>

                      <div className="sm:col-span-6 flex flex-wrap items-center sm:justify-end gap-1.5">
                        <Armchair className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-brand-primary" />
                        <span className="text-xs text-ink-muted">Seats:</span>
                        {seatLabels.length > 0 ? (
                          seatLabels.map((lbl: string, idx: number) => (
                            <span
                              key={idx}
                              className="text-xs font-mono font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-brand-subtle text-brand-primary border border-brand-border"
                            >
                              {lbl}
                            </span>
                          ))
                        ) : (
                          <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-ink-secondary">
                            {passengers.length || 1} Seat(s)
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="pt-3 border-t border-border-hairline flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-ink-muted">
                        Booked: {new Date(booking.createdAt || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString('en-IN')}
                      </span>

                      {isConfirmed ? (
                        <Link
                          href={`/bookings/${bId}`}
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-brand-primary text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-brand-hover shadow-glow transition-all"
                        >
                          <Ticket className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          <span>View Boarding Ticket</span>
                          <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        </Link>
                      ) : isPending ? (
                        <Link
                          href={`/pay/${bId}`}
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-amber-600 transition-all"
                        >
                          <span>Complete Payment</span>
                          <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        </Link>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-xs text-ink-muted font-medium">Inactive</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\app\schedule\[scheduleId]\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth/AuthContext';
import { useWsEvent } from '@/lib/ws/useWsEvent';
import { formatINR, formatISTTime, formatDuration } from '@/lib/utils/formatters';
import {
  fetchScheduleDetail,
  fetchSeatAvailability,
  lockSeat,
  releaseSeatLock,
} from '@/features/seats/api/seats.api';
import { ScheduleDetail, Seat, SeatLayout, ActiveLock } from '@/features/seats/types';
import { SeatDeck } from '@/features/seats/components/SeatDeck';
import { SeatLegend } from '@/features/seats/components/SeatLegend';
import { LockCountdown } from '@/features/seats/components/LockCountdown';
import { AuthModal } from '@/features/auth/components/AuthModal';
import { TransitLoader } from '@/components/brand/TransitLoader';
import { ThemeToggle } from '@/lib/theme/ThemeToggle';
import { BusVerseLogo } from '@/components/brand/BusVerseLogo';
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, AlertCircle, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';

const MAX_SEATS_PER_BOOKING = 6;
type LockMap = Map<string, ActiveLock>;

export default function ScheduleDetailPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ scheduleId: string }> }) {
  const resolvedParams = use(params);
  const scheduleId = resolvedParams.scheduleId;
  const router = useRouter();
  const { isAuthenticated } = useAuth();

  const [schedule, setSchedule] = useState<ScheduleDetail | null>(null);
  const [seats, setSeats] = useState<Seat[]>([]);
  const [layout, setLayout] = useState<SeatLayout | null>(null);
  const [activeLocks, setActiveLocks] = useState<LockMap>(() => new Map());

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [locking, setLocking] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isAuthOpen, setIsAuthOpen] = useState(false);
  const [pendingSeat, setPendingSeat] = useState<Seat | null>(null);

  const loadData = async (): Promise<Seat[]> => {
    setErrorMsg(null);
    try {
      const sched = await fetchScheduleDetail(scheduleId);
      setSchedule(sched);

      const avail = await fetchSeatAvailability(scheduleId, sched.bus?.capacity || 40);
      setSeats(avail.seats);
      setLayout(avail.layout);
      return avail.seats;
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message || 'Unable to load schedule data.');
      return [];
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, [scheduleId]);

  useWsEvent(`seat:availability:${scheduleId}`, 'seat:locked', (data: { seatId: string }) => {
    if (!data?.seatId) return;
    setSeats((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.id === data.seatId && !activeLocks.has(s.id) ? { ...s, status: 'LOCKED' } : s))
    );
  });

  useWsEvent(`seat:availability:${scheduleId}`, 'seat:released', (data: { seatId: string }) => {
    if (!data?.seatId) return;
    setSeats((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.id === data.seatId && !activeLocks.has(s.id) ? { ...s, status: 'AVAILABLE' } : s))
    );
  });

  useWsEvent(`seat:availability:${scheduleId}`, 'seat:booked', (data: { seatId: string }) => {
    if (!data?.seatId) return;
    setSeats((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.id === data.seatId ? { ...s, status: 'BOOKED' } : s))
    );
  });

  const handleSeatClick = async (seat: Seat) => {
    setErrorMsg(null);

    if (activeLocks.has(seat.id)) {
      const existing = activeLocks.get(seat.id)!;
      try {
        await releaseSeatLock(existing.lockId);
      } catch {
        // Ignored
      }
      setActiveLocks((prev) => {
        const next = new Map(prev);
        next.delete(seat.id);
        return next;
      });
      return;
    }

    if (!isAuthenticated) {
      setPendingSeat(seat);
      setIsAuthOpen(true);
      return;
    }

    if (activeLocks.size >= MAX_SEATS_PER_BOOKING) {
      setErrorMsg(`Maximum of ${MAX_SEATS_PER_BOOKING} seats can be selected per booking.`);
      return;
    }

    executeLock(seat);
  };

  const executeLock = async (seat: Seat) => {
    setLocking(true);
    try {
      const lockRes = await lockSeat(scheduleId, seat.id);
      setActiveLocks((prev) => {
        const next = new Map(prev);
        next.set(seat.id, {
          lockId: lockRes.lockId,
          seatId: seat.id,
          seatLabel: seat.seatLabel,
          scheduleId,
          expiresAt: lockRes.expiresAt,
        });
        return next;
      });
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.code === 'SEAT_LOCKED') {
        setErrorMsg(`Seat ${seat.seatLabel} was just taken by another passenger.`);
        setSeats((prev) => prev.map((s) => (s.id === seat.id ? { ...s, status: 'LOCKED' } : s)));
      } else {
        setErrorMsg(err.message || 'Could not lock seat. Please pick another.');
      }
    } finally {
      setLocking(false);
    }
  };

  const handleLockExpire = () => {
    setActiveLocks(new Map());
    setErrorMsg('Your 5-minute seat reservation expired. Please pick your seats again.');
    loadData();
  };

  const handleProceedToBooking = () => {
    if (activeLocks.size === 0) return;
    const lockList = Array.from(activeLocks.values());

    const primaryLockId = lockList[0].lockId;
    const seatIdsParam = lockList.map((l) => l.seatId).join(',');
    const labelsParam = lockList.map((l) => l.seatLabel).join(',');

    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      sessionStorage.setItem('busverse_checkout_locks', JSON.stringify(lockList));
    }

    router.push(`/book/${scheduleId}/${primaryLockId}?seats=${seatIdsParam}&labels=${encodeURIComponent(labelsParam)}`);
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas flex items-center justify-center">
        <TransitLoader caption="SYNCHRONIZING SEAT DECK" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const selectedList = Array.from(activeLocks.values());
  const selectedSeatIds = selectedList.map((l) => l.seatId);

  const farePaise = schedule
    ? typeof schedule.fare === 'object'
      ? schedule.fare.amountPaise
      : schedule.fare
    : 0;
  const totalAmountPaise = farePaise * Math.max(1, selectedList.length);

  const earliestExpiry = selectedList.reduce((earliest, cur) => {
    if (!earliest) return cur.expiresAt;
    return new Date(cur.expiresAt) < new Date(earliest) ? cur.expiresAt : earliest;
  }, '');

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas pb-32">
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-surface-card/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-border-hairline shadow-sm">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 h-16 flex items-center justify-between">
          <Link
            href="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-ink-secondary hover:text-brand-primary transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>All Schedules</span>
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <BusVerseLogo size={32} variant="mark" />
            <ThemeToggle />
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pt-6">
        {schedule && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-5 rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient">
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-3 border-b border-border-hairline">
              <div>
                <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
                  {schedule.route.originCity.name} → {schedule.route.destinationCity.name}
                </h1>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-ink-secondary mt-0.5">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-ink-bright">{schedule.operatorName}</span>
                  <span>•</span>
                  <span>{schedule.busType}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="text-left sm:text-right">
                <div className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-ink-bright">{formatINR(farePaise)}</div>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-ink-muted">per seat (tax included)</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 items-center pt-3 text-xs">
              <div>
                <span className="text-ink-muted block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase">Departs</span>
                <span className="font-extrabold text-sm text-ink-bright">
                  {formatISTTime(schedule.departureTime)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="text-center">
                <span className="text-ink-muted block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase">Duration</span>
                <span className="font-semibold text-ink-secondary">
                  {formatDuration(schedule.durationMinutes)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right">
                <span className="text-ink-muted block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase">Arrives</span>
                <span className="font-extrabold text-sm text-ink-bright">
                  {formatISTTime(schedule.arrivalTime)}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {errorMsg && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-xl bg-danger-subtle border border-danger-crimson text-danger-crimson text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{errorMsg}</span>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => loadData()}
              className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-danger-crimson/10 text-xs inline-flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Retry</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="mb-6">
          <SeatLegend />
        </div>

        {seats.length > 0 && (
          <div className="my-8">
            <SeatDeck
              seats={seats}
              layout={layout || { rowsCount: 10, columnsCount: 4, aisleCells: [] }}
              selectedSeatIds={selectedSeatIds}
              onSeatClick={handleSeatClick}
              disabled={locking}
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40 bg-surface-card/95 backdrop-blur-md border-t border-border-strong shadow-modal p-4">
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 w-full sm:w-auto">
            {selectedList.length > 0 ? (
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                  <span className="text-xs text-ink-secondary">Selected:</span>
                  {selectedList.map((l) => (
                    <span
                      key={l.seatId}
                      className="text-xs font-mono font-extrabold text-brand-primary px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-brand-subtle border border-brand-border"
                    >
                      {l.seatLabel}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink-muted">
                    ({selectedList.length} {selectedList.length === 1 ? 'seat' : 'seats'})
                  </span>
                </div>
                {earliestExpiry && (
                  <div className="mt-1">
                    <LockCountdown expiresAt={earliestExpiry} onExpire={handleLockExpire} />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-ink-muted">
                Tap up to {MAX_SEATS_PER_BOOKING} available seats on the coach deck to reserve
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 w-full sm:w-auto justify-end">
            {selectedList.length > 0 && (
              <div className="text-right">
                <div className="text-xs text-ink-muted">Total Fare</div>
                <div className="text-lg font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
                  {formatINR(totalAmountPaise)}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleProceedToBooking}
              disabled={selectedList.length === 0}
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl bg-brand-primary text-white hover:bg-brand-hover shadow-glow disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-all cursor-pointer"
            >
              <span>Continue ({selectedList.length} {selectedList.length === 1 ? 'Seat' : 'Seats'})</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <AuthModal
        isOpen={isAuthOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsAuthOpen(false)}
        onSuccess={async () => {
          const freshSeats = await loadData();
          if (pendingSeat) {
            const realMatch = freshSeats.find((s) => s.seatLabel === pendingSeat.seatLabel) || pendingSeat;
            executeLock(realMatch);
            setPendingSeat(null);
          }
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\app\trips\[tripId]\live\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useWsEvent } from '@/lib/ws/useWsEvent';
import { fetchLiveTripSnapshot } from '@/features/tickets/api/tickets.api';
import { LiveTripSnapshot } from '@/features/tickets/types';
import { LiveTelemetryHud } from '@/features/telemetry/components/LiveTelemetryHud';
import { TransitLoader } from '@/components/brand/TransitLoader';
import { ThemeToggle } from '@/lib/theme/ThemeToggle';
import { BusVerseLogo } from '@/components/brand/BusVerseLogo';
import { ArrowLeft, AlertCircle, Info, Smartphone } from 'lucide-react';

export default function LiveTripTrackingPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ tripId: string }>;
}) {
  const resolvedParams = use(params);
  const tripId = resolvedParams.tripId;

  const [snapshot, setSnapshot] = useState<LiveTripSnapshot | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const loadSnapshot = async () => {
    try {
      const data = await fetchLiveTripSnapshot(tripId);
      setSnapshot(data);
      setErrorMsg(null);
    } catch {
      setErrorMsg('Live tracking is currently unavailable for this trip. It may not have started yet.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadSnapshot();

    // 3-second polling fallback if WebSocket is offline
    const pollTimer = setInterval(() => {
      fetchLiveTripSnapshot(tripId)
        .then((fresh) => {
          setSnapshot(fresh);
          setErrorMsg(null);
        })
        .catch(() => { /* ignore polling errors, rely on initial error */ });
    }, 3000);

    return () => clearInterval(pollTimer);
  }, [tripId]);

  // Real-time WebSocket: Subscribe to trip:live:{tripId}
  useWsEvent(`trip:live:${tripId}`, 'bus:position', (data: { lat: number; lng: number; speedKmh: number }) => {
    setSnapshot((prev) => {
      if (!prev) return null;
      return {
        ...prev,
        status: 'IN_PROGRESS',
        gps: {
          lat: data.lat,
          lng: data.lng,
          speedKmh: data.speedKmh,
          recordedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        },
      };
    });
  });

  useWsEvent(`trip:live:${tripId}`, 'trip:delayed', (data: { delayMinutes: number }) => {
    setSnapshot((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, delayMinutes: data.delayMinutes } : null));
  });

  useWsEvent(`trip:live:${tripId}`, 'trip:ended', () => {
    setSnapshot((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, status: 'COMPLETED' } : null));
  });

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas flex items-center justify-center">
        <TransitLoader caption="SYNCHRONIZING VEHICLE TELEMETRY" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas pb-20">
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-surface-card/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-border-hairline shadow-sm">
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 h-16 flex items-center justify-between">
          <Link
            href="/bookings"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-ink-secondary hover:text-brand-primary transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>My Bookings</span>
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <BusVerseLogo size={32} variant="mark" />
            <ThemeToggle />
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pt-6 space-y-4">
        
        {/* Disclaimer Banner */}
        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-500 text-sm flex items-start gap-3">
          <Info size={20} className="shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div>
            <p className="font-semibold">Demo Environment Requirement</p>
            <p className="text-xs mt-1 text-amber-600/80">
              Live tracking requires a real driver streaming GPS. To see the map move, open the
              <strong> Operator App (Driver)</strong>, go to this trip, and click <strong>"Start Trip"</strong>.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {errorMsg && !snapshot ? (
          <div className="p-8 rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-sm text-center">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 mx-auto bg-surface-sunken rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
              <Smartphone className="w-8 h-8 text-ink-muted" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-ink-bright mb-2">Waiting for Driver</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-ink-secondary mb-6 max-w-md mx-auto">
              {errorMsg}
            </p>
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-surface-sunken text-xs font-mono text-ink-muted border border-border-subtle">
              <AlertCircle size={14} />
              Status: Awaiting Trip Start
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : snapshot ? (
          <LiveTelemetryHud snapshot={snapshot} />
        ) : null}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\components\brand\BusVerseLogo.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React from 'react';

interface BusVerseLogoProps {
  className?: string;
  size?: number | string;
  variant?: 'full' | 'mark' | 'wordmark';
}

/**
 * BusVerse Master Vector Mark (1254x1254 native coordinate space)
 * Dynamically adapts color fills to light/dark themes via CSS tokens.
 */
export function BusVerseLogo({
  className = '',
  size = 48,
  variant = 'full',
}: BusVerseLogoProps) {
  // If variant is 'mark', we restrict the viewBox to the upper vehicle + pin region
  const viewBox = variant === 'mark' ? '320 220 740 560' : '0 0 1254 1254';

  return (
    <svg
      viewBox={viewBox}
      width={size}
      height={size}
      className={`inline-block transition-transform duration-200 ${className}`}
      fill="none"
      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
      aria-label="BusVerse Brand Mark"
    >
      {/* Road / Speed Ribbon (Electric Blue) */}
      {(variant === 'full' || variant === 'mark') && (
        <path
          fill="var(--logo-primary)"
          fillRule="evenodd"
          d="M 332,609 L 332,627 L 340,645 L 360,667 L 392,690 L 433,712 L 471,728 L 504,739 L 554,752 L 595,759 L 634,763 L 721,764 L 765,760 L 818,752 L 865,741 L 888,733 L 702,690 L 570,652 L 493,623 L 460,605 L 444,590 L 440,580 L 441,572 L 445,565 L 455,556 L 474,546 L 496,538 L 555,523 L 517,526 L 474,533 L 435,543 L 404,554 L 372,569 L 353,581 L 338,596 Z M 576,718 L 596,707 L 698,735 L 685,743 L 674,747 L 603,728 Z M 458,673 L 471,666 L 479,664 L 548,693 L 527,704 L 485,687 Z M 392,624 L 409,619 L 421,631 L 448,648 L 427,656 L 410,644 Z M 402,580 L 404,582 L 399,595 L 401,606 L 387,610 L 385,605 L 387,594 L 397,580 Z"
        />
      )}

      {/* Bus Body Silhouette & Shadow */}
      {(variant === 'full' || variant === 'mark') && (
        <g fill="var(--logo-vehicle)">
          <path
            fillRule="evenodd"

            <path d="M 204,286 L 254,286 A 38,38 0 0 1 332,286 L 476,286 A 38,38 0 0 1 554,286 L 602,286" fill="none" stroke="#00F0FF" strokeWidth="2" opacity="0.85" />

            {/* Tinted Windshield & Windows */}
            <path d="M 220,188 L 535,188 Q 552,188 563,200 L 579,222 Q 586,232 588,246 L 589,252 L 220,252 Z" fill="url(#glassGrad)" stroke="#0B1830" strokeWidth="2" />
            <path d="M 280,252 L 325,188 L 350,188 L 305,252 Z" fill="url(#glassSheen)" />
            <path d="M 440,252 L 485,188 L 500,188 L 455,252 Z" fill="url(#glassSheen)" opacity="0.5" />

            <line x1="275" y1="188" x2="275" y2="252" stroke="#0B1830" strokeWidth="3" />

            <line x1="480" y1="188" x2="480" y2="252" stroke="#0B1830" strokeWidth="3" />

                <circle cx="293" cy="310" r="19" fill="url(#rimGrad)" />
                <circle cx="293" cy="310" r="13" fill="none" stroke="#00E5FF" strokeWidth="2" strokeDasharray="6 7" />
                <circle cx="293" cy="310" r="5" fill="#0055ED" />
              </g>
            </g>

            {/* Front Wheel Assembly */}
            <g>
              <path d="M 480,310 A 35,35 0 0 1 550,310 Z" fill="#050912" />
              <circle cx="515" cy="310" r="28" fill="#111824" stroke="#070C14" strokeWidth="2" />
              <g className="loader-wheel">
                <circle cx="515" cy="310" r="19" fill="url(#rimGrad)" />
                <circle cx="515" cy="310" r="13" fill="none" stroke="#00E5FF" strokeWidth="2" strokeDasharray="6 7" />
                <circle cx="515" cy="310" r="5" fill="#0055ED" />
              </g>
            </g>
          </g>

          {/* Telemetry Status Dots */}
          <g transform="translate(400, 435)">
            <text
              className="loader-status-text"
              textAnchor="middle"
              fontFamily="Plus Jakarta Sans, -apple-system, sans-serif"
              fontSize="14"
              fontWeight="700"
              letterSpacing="6"
            >
              {caption}
              <tspan className="loading-dot-1">.</tspan>
              <tspan className="loading-dot-2">.</tspan>
              <tspan className="loading-dot-3">.</tspan>
            </text>
          </g>
        </svg>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  if (size === 'fullscreen') {
    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-canvas/80 backdrop-blur-md">
        {loaderContent}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return loaderContent;
}

'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth/AuthContext';
import { BusVerseLogo } from '@/components/brand/BusVerseLogo';
import { ThemeToggle } from '@/lib/theme/ThemeToggle';
import { AuthModal } from '@/features/auth/components/AuthModal';
import { User, Ticket, LogIn } from 'lucide-react';

interface AppHeaderProps {
  backHref?: string;
  backLabel?: string;
}

export function AppHeader({ backHref, backLabel }: AppHeaderProps) {
  const { user, isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
  const [isAuthOpen, setIsAuthOpen] = useState(false);

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-surface-card/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-border-hairline shadow-sm">
      <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 h-16 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <BusVerseLogo size={32} variant="mark" />
            <span className="font-extrabold text-base tracking-tight text-ink-bright hidden sm:inline">
              BusVerse
            </span>
          </Link>

          {backHref && (
            <Link
              href={backHref}
              className="text-xs font-semibold text-ink-secondary hover:text-brand-primary transition-colors border-l border-border-hairline pl-3"
            >
              {backLabel || '← Back'}
            </Link>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <ThemeToggle />

          {isAuthenticated ? (
            <Link
              href="/profile"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-brand-subtle border border-brand-border text-brand-primary hover:bg-brand-primary hover:text-white transition-all text-xs font-mono font-bold cursor-pointer"
            >
              <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span className="max-w-[120px] truncate">{user?.name || user?.phone || 'Profile'}</span>
              <Ticket className="w-3 h-3 opacity-70 ml-0.5" />
            </Link>
          ) : (
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsAuthOpen(true)}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-brand-primary text-white hover:bg-brand-hover text-xs font-bold shadow-sm transition-all cursor-pointer"
            >
              <LogIn className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Sign In</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <AuthModal isOpen={isAuthOpen} onClose={() => setIsAuthOpen(false)} />
    </header>
  );
}
