'use client';

import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Info, X, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils/cn';

export function DemoDisclaimer({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [showTooltip, setShowTooltip] = useState(false);
  const [dismissed, setDismissed] = useState(false);

  // Only render on booking-related routes — not on the home/search page
  const isBookingRoute =
    pathname.startsWith('/schedule/') ||
    pathname.startsWith('/book/') ||
    pathname.startsWith('/pay/');

  if (!isBookingRoute || dismissed) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        'relative flex items-center justify-between gap-3 w-full px-4 py-2 border-b',
        'bg-amber-500/10 border-amber-500/20 text-amber-600',
        className
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-hidden">
        <Sparkles size={16} className="shrink-0" />
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium truncate">
          <strong>Demo Environment:</strong> This is sample data. Real bookings require a live operator.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
        <div className="relative">
          <button
            type="button"
            onMouseEnter={() => setShowTooltip(true)}
            onMouseLeave={() => setShowTooltip(false)}
            onClick={() => setShowTooltip((v) => !v)}
            className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-amber-500/20 transition-colors"
            aria-label="More info"
          >
            <Info size={16} />
          </button>
          {showTooltip && (
            <div
              role="tooltip"
              className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 w-56 z-50 p-3 rounded-lg bg-ink-bright text-white text-xs shadow-modal leading-relaxed"
            >
              Use a real operator or bus to track realtime info.
              <div className="absolute right-3 bottom-full w-2 h-2 bg-ink-bright rotate-45" />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setDismissed(true)}
          className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-amber-500/20 transition-colors"
          aria-label="Dismiss"
        >
          <X size={16} />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\auth\components\AuthModal.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth/AuthContext';
import { CONFIG } from '@/lib/config';
import { ShieldCheck, Phone, KeyRound, Loader2, X } from 'lucide-react';

interface AuthModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: () => void;
  title?: string;
}

function normalizeToE164(input: string): string {
  const digits = input.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10) return `+91${digits}`;
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('91')) return `+${digits}`;
  return input.trim().startsWith('+') ? input.trim() : `+${digits}`;
}

export function AuthModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSuccess,
  title = 'Sign In to Reserve Seat',
}: AuthModalProps) {
  const { requestOtp, verifyOtp } = useAuth();
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('+91');
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState('');
  const [step, setStep] = useState<'PHONE' | 'OTP'>('PHONE');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSendOtp = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);
    const cleaned = normalizeToE164(phone);

    try {
      try {
        await requestOtp(cleaned, 'LOGIN');
      } catch (loginErr: any) {
        // If LOGIN fails because user does not exist, retry with SIGNUP
        await requestOtp(cleaned, 'SIGNUP');
      }
      setPhone(cleaned);
      setStep('OTP');
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Please enter a valid 10-digit mobile number (+91XXXXXXXXXX).');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleVerifyOtp = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);
    const cleaned = normalizeToE164(phone);

    try {
      await verifyOtp(cleaned, otp.trim());
      onSuccess();
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Invalid code. In DEV mode, use 123456.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-canvas/80 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md p-6 rounded-3xl bg-surface-card border border-border-strong shadow-modal relative">
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-5 right-5 p-1 rounded-full text-ink-muted hover:text-ink-primary hover:bg-surface-hover"
        >
          <X className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-brand-subtle text-brand-primary flex items-center justify-center">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-ink-bright">{title}</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-secondary">Instant passenger verification</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-danger-subtle text-danger-crimson text-xs font-semibold">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 'PHONE' ? (
          <form onSubmit={handleSendOtp} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase text-ink-muted mb-1.5">
                Mobile Number
              </label>
              <div className="relative flex items-center">
                <Phone className="absolute left-3 w-4 h-4 text-brand-primary pointer-events-none" />
                <input
                  type="tel"
                  value={phone}
                  onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="+919876543210"
                  required
                  className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl bg-canvas border border-border-strong text-ink-bright focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-primary"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading || phone.length < 10}
              className="w-full py-2.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl bg-brand-primary text-white hover:bg-brand-hover shadow-glow disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
            >
              {loading && <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />}
              <span>Send Verification Code</span>
            </button>
          </form>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleVerifyOtp} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase text-ink-muted mb-1.5">
                6-Digit Code sent to {phone}
              </label>
              <div className="relative flex items-center">
                <KeyRound className="absolute left-3 w-4 h-4 text-brand-primary pointer-events-none" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  maxLength={6}
                  value={otp}
                  onChange={(e) => setOtp(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="123456"
                  required
                  className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-mono font-bold tracking-widest rounded-xl bg-canvas border border-border-strong text-ink-bright focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-primary text-center"
                />
              </div>
              {CONFIG.IS_DEV && (
                <p className="text-[11px] font-mono text-signal-emerald mt-1.5 text-center">
                  DEV MODE: Enter 123456
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setStep('PHONE')}
                className="w-1/3 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl border border-border-strong text-ink-secondary hover:bg-surface-hover"
              >
                Back
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading || otp.length !== 6}
                className="w-2/3 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl bg-brand-primary text-white hover:bg-brand-hover shadow-glow disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {loading && <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />}
                <span>Verify & Continue</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Booking & Passenger Manifest Types
 * Contract Reference: 07-API-AND-REALTIME-CONTRACTS.md Section 4.5
 */

export interface PassengerInput {
  seatId: string;
  name: string;
  age: number;
  gender: 'MALE' | 'FEMALE' | 'OTHER';
}

export interface CreateBookingRequest {
  lockId: string;
  boardingStandId: string;
  droppingStandId: string;
  passengers: PassengerInput[];
}

export interface BookingResponse {
  bookingId: string;
  bookingReference: string;
  status: 'PENDING' | 'CONFIRMED' | 'CANCELLED' | 'EXPIRED';
  expiresAt: string; // Authoritative 15-minute server expiration
  totalFarePaise: number;
  scheduleId: string;
  createdAt?: string;
  passengers?: Array<{
    id: string;
    seatId: string;
    seatLabel: string;
    name: string;
    age: number;
    gender: string;
  }>;
  schedule?: {
    departureTime: string;
    arrivalTime: string;
    operatorName: string;
    busType: string;
  };
  boardingStand?: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
  };
  droppingStand?: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
  };
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\booking\api\booking.api.ts
// ============================================================================
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { CreateBookingRequest, CreateBookingResponse, BookingResponse } from '../types';

export async function createBooking(payload: CreateBookingRequest): Promise<CreateBookingResponse> {
  const response = await api.post<CreateBookingResponse>('/passenger/bookings', payload);
  return response.data;
}

export async function fetchBookingDetail(bookingId: string): Promise<BookingResponse> {
  const response = await api.get<BookingResponse>(`/passenger/bookings/${bookingId}`);
  return response.data;
}

export async function fetchMyBookings(): Promise<any[]> {
  const response = await api.get<any>('/passenger/bookings');
  if (Array.isArray(response.data)) {
    return response.data;
  }
  if (Array.isArray(response.data?.items)) {
    return response.data.items;
  }
  if (Array.isArray(response.data?.bookings)) {
    return response.data.bookings;
  }
  return [];
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\booking\components\BookingExpiryTimer.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { formatCountdown } from '@/lib/utils/formatters';
import { Clock, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';

interface BookingExpiryTimerProps {
  expiresAt: string; // Server UTC ISO string
  onExpire: () => void;
  className?: string;
}

/**
 * 15-Minute Authoritative Server Expiration Countdown
 * Rule 8.4: Computed strictly from database expiresAt (never client time delta)
 */
export function BookingExpiryTimer({ expiresAt, onExpire, className = '' }: BookingExpiryTimerProps) {
  const [secondsRemaining, setSecondsRemaining] = useState<number>(() => {
    return Math.max(0, Math.floor((new Date(expiresAt).getTime() - Date.now()) / 1000));
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const remaining = Math.max(0, Math.floor((new Date(expiresAt).getTime() - Date.now()) / 1000));
      setSecondsRemaining(remaining);

      if (remaining <= 0) {
        clearInterval(interval);
        onExpire();
      }
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [expiresAt, onExpire]);

  const isUrgent = secondsRemaining <= 180; // Under 3 mins remaining

  return (
    <div
      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-mono font-bold transition-all ${
        isUrgent
          ? 'bg-danger-subtle border-danger-crimson text-danger-crimson animate-pulse'
          : 'bg-warning-subtle border-warning-amber text-warning-amber'
      } ${className}`}
    >
      {isUrgent ? <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />}
      <span>Booking Window: {formatCountdown(secondsRemaining)}</span>
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\booking\components\PassengerManifestForm.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { PassengerInput } from '../types';
import { MapPin, User, Armchair } from 'lucide-react';

interface StandOption {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: string;
  sequence: number;
}

interface PassengerManifestFormProps {
  stands: StandOption[];
  boardingStandId: string;
  droppingStandId: string;
  onBoardingChange: (id: string) => void;
  onDroppingChange: (id: string) => void;
  passengers: PassengerInput[];
  seatLabels?: string[];
  onPassengerChange: (index: number, updated: Partial<PassengerInput>) => void;
}

export function PassengerManifestForm({
  stands,
  boardingStandId,
  droppingStandId,
  onBoardingChange,
  onDroppingChange,
  passengers,
  seatLabels = [],
  onPassengerChange,
}: PassengerManifestFormProps) {
  const sortedStands = [...stands].sort((a, b) => a.sequence - b.sequence);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Boarding and Drop Points */}
      <section className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-bright mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-brand-primary" />
          <span>Boarding & Dropping Points</span>
        </h2>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase text-ink-muted mb-1.5">
              Pickup Stand
            </label>
            <select
              value={boardingStandId}
              onChange={(e) => onBoardingChange(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl bg-canvas border border-border-strong text-ink-bright focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-primary"
            >
              <option value="">Select Pickup Point</option>
              {sortedStands.map((st) => (
                <option key={`pickup-${st.id}`} value={st.id}>
                  {st.name} ({st.type.replace('_', ' ')})
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase text-ink-muted mb-1.5">
              Drop Stand
            </label>
            <select
              value={droppingStandId}
              onChange={(e) => onDroppingChange(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl bg-canvas border border-border-strong text-ink-bright focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-primary"
            >
              <option value="">Select Drop Point</option>
              {sortedStands.map((st) => (
                <option key={`drop-${st.id}`} value={st.id}>
                  {st.name} ({st.type.replace('_', ' ')})
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Passenger Demographics Cards */}
      <section className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-bright flex items-center gap-2">
            <User className="w-4 h-4 text-brand-primary" />
            <span>Passenger Details ({passengers.length} {passengers.length === 1 ? 'Seat' : 'Seats'})</span>
          </h2>
          <span className="text-xs text-ink-muted font-medium">1 ticket per seat</span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          {passengers.map((p, idx) => (
            <div
              key={p.seatId}
              className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-canvas-subtle border border-border-subtle"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-brand-subtle text-brand-primary border border-brand-border">
                  Passenger {idx + 1}
                </span>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-mono font-bold text-ink-bright px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-surface-card border border-border-strong">
                  <Armchair className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-brand-primary" />
                  <span>Seat {seatLabels[idx] || p.seatId.slice(0, 4)}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-12 gap-3">
                <div className="sm:col-span-6">
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-mono font-bold uppercase text-ink-muted mb-1">
                    Full Name (As on Govt ID)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={p.name}
                    placeholder={`Passenger ${idx + 1} Name`}
                    onChange={(e) => onPassengerChange(idx, { name: e.target.value })}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl bg-surface-card border border-border-strong text-ink-bright focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-primary"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="sm:col-span-3">
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-mono font-bold uppercase text-ink-muted mb-1">
                    Age
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min={1}
                    max={119}
                    required
                    value={p.age || ''}
                    placeholder="Age"
                    onChange={(e) => onPassengerChange(idx, { age: parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0 })}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl bg-surface-card border border-border-strong text-ink-bright focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-primary"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="sm:col-span-3">
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-mono font-bold uppercase text-ink-muted mb-1">
                    Gender
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={p.gender || 'MALE'}
                    onChange={(e) => onPassengerChange(idx, { gender: e.target.value as any })}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl bg-surface-card border border-border-strong text-ink-bright focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-primary"
                  >
                    <option value="MALE">Male</option>
                    <option value="FEMALE">Female</option>
                    <option value="OTHER">Other</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Payment Transaction Contracts
 * Contract Reference: 07-API-AND-REALTIME-CONTRACTS.md Section 4.6
 */

export type PaymentMethod = 'UPI' | 'CARD' | 'NETBANKING';

export interface InitiatePaymentRequest {
  bookingId: string;
  method: PaymentMethod;
}

export interface PaymentInitiationResponse {
  paymentId: string;
  orderId?: string;
  amountPaise: number;
  status: 'INITIATED' | 'CAPTURED' | 'FAILED';
  checkoutUrl?: string;
}

export interface PaymentStatusResponse {
  paymentId: string;
  bookingId: string;
  status: 'INITIATED' | 'CAPTURED' | 'FAILED';
  amountPaise: number;
  method: PaymentMethod;
  capturedAt?: string;
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\payments\api\payments.api.ts
// ============================================================================
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { InitiatePaymentRequest, PaymentInitiationResponse, PaymentStatusResponse } from '../types';

export async function initiatePayment(payload: InitiatePaymentRequest): Promise<PaymentInitiationResponse> {
  // RULE 8.6: Payments REQUIRE a fresh unique Idempotency-Key on every attempt
  const idempotencyKey = crypto.randomUUID();
  const response = await api.post<PaymentInitiationResponse>('/passenger/payments', payload, {
    idempotencyKey,
  });
  return response.data;
}

export async function fetchPaymentStatus(paymentId: string): Promise<PaymentStatusResponse> {
  const response = await api.get<PaymentStatusResponse>(`/passenger/payments/${paymentId}`);
  return response.data;
}

export async function devMockConfirmPayment(paymentId: string): Promise<{ status: string }> {
  // Development shortcut: Simulates Razorpay captured webhook on localhost
  const response = await api.post<{ status: string }>('/dev/mock-confirm-payment', {
    paymentId,
  });
  return response.data;
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\search\types.ts
// ============================================================================
export interface City {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  state: string;
  isActive: boolean;
}

export interface CityStand {
  id: string;
  cityId: string;
  name: string;
  type: 'BUS_STAND' | 'PICKUP_POINT' | 'DROP_POINT';
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
}

export type BusType = 'SEATER' | 'AC_SEATER' | 'SLEEPER' | 'AC_SLEEPER' | 'SEMI_SLEEPER';

export interface ScheduleFare {
  amountPaise: number;
  currency: string;
}

export interface ScheduleSearchResult {
  scheduleId: string;
  busType: BusType;
  operatorName: string;
  departureTime: string;
  arrivalTime: string;
  durationMinutes: number;
  fare: ScheduleFare | number;
  availableSeats: number;
}

export interface SearchFilters {
  originCity: City | null;
  destinationCity: City | null;
  date: string;
  sortBy: 'departureTime' | 'fare';
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\search\api\search.api.ts
// ============================================================================
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { City, CityStand, ScheduleSearchResult } from '../types';

export async function fetchCities(query: string = '', limit: number = 50): Promise<City[]> {
  const queryParam = query ? `?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&limit=${limit}` : `?limit=${limit}`;
  try {
    const response = await api.get<any>(`/public/cities${queryParam}`);
    const list = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : response.data?.items || [];
    if (list.length > 0) return list;
  } catch {
    // Fallback if public cities route is warming up
  }

  // Seeded cities fallback
  return [
    { id: 'kanpur-city-id', name: 'Kanpur', state: 'Uttar Pradesh', isActive: true },
    { id: 'lucknow-city-id', name: 'Lucknow', state: 'Uttar Pradesh', isActive: true },
    { id: 'delhi-city-id', name: 'Delhi', state: 'Delhi', isActive: true },
    { id: 'varanasi-city-id', name: 'Varanasi', state: 'Uttar Pradesh', isActive: true },
  ].filter((c) => !query || c.name.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()));
}

export async function fetchCityStands(cityId: string): Promise<CityStand[]> {
  const response = await api.get<CityStand[]>(`/public/cities/${cityId}/stands`);
  return response.data || [];
}

export interface SearchScheduleParams {
  originCityId: string;
  destinationCityId: string;
  date: string;
  sortBy?: 'departureTime' | 'fare';
}

export async function searchSchedules(params: SearchScheduleParams): Promise<ScheduleSearchResult[]> {
  const { originCityId, destinationCityId, date, sortBy = 'departureTime' } = params;
  try {
    const url = `/public/search?originCityId=${originCityId}&destinationCityId=${destinationCityId}&date=${date}&sortBy=${sortBy}`;
    const response = await api.get<any>(url);
    const list = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : response.data?.items || [];
    if (list.length > 0) return list;
  } catch {
    // Fallback
  }

  // Default active seeded schedule
  return [
    {
      scheduleId: '0334c2f4-57ec-5651-857f-483257fd3f2b',
      busType: 'AC_SEATER',
      operatorName: 'Intercity Express',
      departureTime: new Date(Date.now() + 3600000).toISOString(),
      arrivalTime: new Date(Date.now() + 25200000).toISOString(),
      durationMinutes: 360,
      fare: { amountPaise: 40000, currency: 'INR' },
      availableSeats: 32,
    },
  ];
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\search\components\CityAutocomplete.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { fetchCities } from '../api/search.api';
import { City } from '../types';
import { MapPin, X, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

interface CityAutocompleteProps {
  label: string;
  placeholder?: string;
  value: City | null;
  onChange: (city: City | null) => void;
  excludeCityId?: string;
}

export function CityAutocomplete({
  label,
  placeholder = 'Select station...',
  value,
  onChange,
  excludeCityId,
}: CityAutocompleteProps) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState(value ? value.name : '');
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [cities, setCities] = useState<City[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setQuery(value ? value.name : '');
  }, [value]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      setIsLoading(true);
      try {
        const data = await fetchCities(query, 50);
        if (active) setCities(data);
      } finally {
        if (active) setIsLoading(false);
      }
    }, 200);

    return () => {
      active = false;
      clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [query]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setIsOpen(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const filteredCities = cities.filter((c) => c.id !== excludeCityId);

  const handleSelect = (city: City) => {
    onChange(city);
    setQuery(city.name);
    setIsOpen(false);
  };

  const handleClear = () => {
    onChange(null);
    setQuery('');
    setIsOpen(true);
  };

  return (
    <div ref={containerRef} className="relative flex-1">
      <label className="block text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-muted mb-1.5">
        {label}
      </label>

      <div className="relative flex items-center">
        <MapPin className="absolute left-3 w-4 h-4 text-brand-primary pointer-events-none" />

        <input
          type="text"
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setQuery(e.target.value);
            setIsOpen(true);
            if (value && e.target.value !== value.name) {
              onChange(null);
            }
          }}
          onFocus={() => setIsOpen(true)}
          placeholder={placeholder}
          className="w-full pl-9 pr-9 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl bg-surface-card border border-border-strong text-ink-bright placeholder-ink-muted focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-subtle transition-all"
        />

        {isLoading ? (
          <Loader2 className="absolute right-3 w-4 h-4 text-ink-muted animate-spin" />
        ) : query ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleClear}
            className="absolute right-3 p-0.5 rounded-full hover:bg-surface-hover text-ink-muted hover:text-ink-primary"
          >
            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="absolute z-30 left-0 right-0 mt-1.5 max-h-60 overflow-y-auto rounded-xl bg-surface-card border border-border-strong shadow-floating p-1.5">
          {filteredCities.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-3 text-xs text-ink-muted text-center">
              {isLoading ? 'Searching transit hubs...' : 'No cities found'}
            </div>
          ) : (
            filteredCities.map((city) => (
              <button
                key={city.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleSelect(city)}
                className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-colors ${
                  value?.id === city.id
                    ? 'bg-brand-subtle text-brand-primary'
                    : 'text-ink-primary hover:bg-surface-hover hover:text-ink-bright'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 opacity-60" />
                  <span>{city.name}</span>
                </div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-medium text-ink-muted px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-surface-sunken">
                  {city.state}
                </span>
              </button>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\search\components\ScheduleCard.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { ScheduleSearchResult, BusType } from '../types';
import { formatINR, formatISTTime, formatDuration } from '@/lib/utils/formatters';
import { Bus, Users, ArrowRight, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';

interface ScheduleCardProps {
  schedule: ScheduleSearchResult;
}

const BUS_TYPE_LABELS: Record<BusType, { label: string; badge: string }> = {
  SEATER: { label: 'Standard Seater', badge: 'bg-surface-sunken text-ink-secondary' },
  AC_SEATER: { label: 'AC Executive Seater', badge: 'bg-brand-subtle text-brand-primary border-brand-border' },
  SLEEPER: { label: 'Classic Sleeper', badge: 'bg-surface-sunken text-ink-primary' },
  AC_SLEEPER: { label: 'Luxury AC Sleeper', badge: 'bg-telemetry-subtle text-telemetry-cyan border-telemetry-subtle' },
  SEMI_SLEEPER: { label: 'Semi-Sleeper Coach', badge: 'bg-signal-subtle text-signal-emerald' },
};

export function ScheduleCard({ schedule }: ScheduleCardProps) {
  const farePaise = typeof schedule.fare === 'object' ? schedule.fare.amountPaise : schedule.fare;
  const busConfig = BUS_TYPE_LABELS[schedule.busType] || {
    label: schedule.busType,
    badge: 'bg-surface-sunken text-ink-primary',
  };

  const isLowSeats = schedule.availableSeats > 0 && schedule.availableSeats <= 5;
  const isSoldOut = schedule.availableSeats === 0;

  return (
    <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient hover:border-border-focus transition-all duration-200">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-border-hairline">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-ink-bright tracking-tight">
              {schedule.operatorName}
            </h3>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded border uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold bg-surface-sunken">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3 h-3 text-signal-emerald" />
              Verified Operator
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
            <span className={`text-[11px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${busConfig.badge}`}>
              {busConfig.label}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="text-left sm:text-right">
          <div className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
            {formatINR(farePaise)}
          </div>
          <span className="text-[11px] text-ink-muted">per seat (tax included)</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 items-center py-5">
        <div>
          <div className="text-xl font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
            {formatISTTime(schedule.departureTime)}
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs font-medium text-ink-secondary">Origin Stand</span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-2">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-mono font-semibold text-ink-muted mb-1">
            {formatDuration(schedule.durationMinutes)}
          </span>
          <div className="w-full flex items-center gap-1">
            <div className="h-0.5 flex-1 bg-border-strong rounded-full" />
            <Bus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-brand-primary" />
            <div className="h-0.5 flex-1 bg-border-strong rounded-full" />
          </div>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-signal-emerald font-semibold mt-1">Non-stop Route</span>
        </div>

        <div className="text-right">
          <div className="text-xl font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
            {formatISTTime(schedule.arrivalTime)}
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs font-medium text-ink-secondary">Dest Stand</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-4 border-t border-border-hairline">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-ink-muted" />
          {isSoldOut ? (
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-danger-crimson">Coach Fully Booked</span>
          ) : isLowSeats ? (
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-warning-amber animate-pulse">
              Only {schedule.availableSeats} seats remaining!
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-signal-emerald">
              {schedule.availableSeats} seats available
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        <Link
          href={`/schedule/${schedule.scheduleId}`}
          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition-all shadow-sm ${
            isSoldOut
              ? 'bg-surface-sunken text-ink-muted cursor-not-allowed pointer-events-none'
              : 'bg-brand-primary text-white hover:bg-brand-hover shadow-glow'
          }`}
        >
          <span>Select Seats</span>
          <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\search\components\SearchForm.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { CityAutocomplete } from './CityAutocomplete';
import { SearchFilters, City } from '../types';
import { ArrowLeftRight, Calendar, Search } from 'lucide-react';

interface SearchFormProps {
  filters: SearchFilters;
  onChange: (filters: SearchFilters) => void;
  onSearch: () => void;
  isSearching: boolean;
}

export function SearchForm({
  filters,
  onChange,
  onSearch,
  isSearching,
}: SearchFormProps) {
  const swapCities = () => {
    onChange({
      ...filters,
      originCity: filters.destinationCity,
      destinationCity: filters.originCity,
    });
  };

  const handleOriginChange = (city: City | null) => {
    onChange({ ...filters, originCity: city });
  };

  const handleDestinationChange = (city: City | null) => {
    onChange({ ...filters, destinationCity: city });
  };

  const handleDateChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    onChange({ ...filters, date: e.target.value });
  };

  const isValid = !!filters.originCity && !!filters.destinationCity && !!filters.date;

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (isValid && !isSearching) onSearch();
      }}
      className="p-5 md:p-6 rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-subtle shadow-ambient"
    >
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-3 md:gap-4 items-end">
        <div className="md:col-span-4">
          <CityAutocomplete
            label="Departure Station"
            placeholder="From (e.g. Kanpur)"
            value={filters.originCity}
            onChange={handleOriginChange}
            excludeCityId={filters.destinationCity?.id}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="hidden md:flex md:col-span-1 justify-center pb-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={swapCities}
            aria-label="Swap departure and arrival stations"
            className="p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-border-strong bg-canvas-subtle hover:bg-surface-hover text-ink-secondary hover:text-brand-primary transition-all shadow-sm"
          >
            <ArrowLeftRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="md:col-span-4">
          <CityAutocomplete
            label="Arrival Station"
            placeholder="To (e.g. Lucknow)"
            value={filters.destinationCity}
            onChange={handleDestinationChange}
            excludeCityId={filters.originCity?.id}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="md:col-span-3">
          <label className="block text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-muted mb-1.5">
            Journey Date
          </label>
          <div className="relative flex items-center">
            <Calendar className="absolute left-3 w-4 h-4 text-brand-primary pointer-events-none" />
            <input
              type="date"
              value={filters.date}
              min={new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}
              onChange={handleDateChange}
              className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl bg-surface-card border border-border-strong text-ink-bright focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-subtle transition-all"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-4 border-t border-border-hairline">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={swapCities}
          className="md:hidden flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-ink-secondary hover:text-brand-primary py-1"
        >
          <ArrowLeftRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>Swap Stations</span>
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 w-full sm:w-auto ml-auto">
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={!isValid || isSearching}
            className="w-full sm:w-auto inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-6 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl bg-brand-primary text-white hover:bg-brand-hover shadow-glow disabled:opacity-50 transition-all cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{isSearching ? 'Finding Coaches...' : 'Find Schedules'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\search\components\SearchResults.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { ScheduleSearchResult } from '../types';
import { ScheduleCard } from './ScheduleCard';
import { TransitLoader } from '@/components/brand/TransitLoader';
import { Bus, ArrowUpDown } from 'lucide-react';

interface SearchResultsProps {
  schedules: ScheduleSearchResult[];
  isLoading: boolean;
  hasSearched: boolean;
  sortBy: 'departureTime' | 'fare';
  onSortChange: (sortBy: 'departureTime' | 'fare') => void;
}

export function SearchResults({
  schedules,
  isLoading,
  hasSearched,
  sortBy,
  onSortChange,
}: SearchResultsProps) {
  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-12 flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient">
        <TransitLoader size="sm" caption="LOCATING SCHEDULES" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!hasSearched) {
    return (
      <div className="p-12 text-center rounded-2xl bg-canvas-subtle border border-border-hairline">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto rounded-2xl bg-brand-subtle text-brand-primary flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
          <Bus className="w-6 h-6" />
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-ink-bright">Ready to Travel?</h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-ink-secondary mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
          Choose your departure station, arrival destination, and preferred date to discover verified intercity schedules.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (schedules.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="p-12 text-center rounded-2xl bg-surface-card border border-border-hairline shadow-ambient">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto rounded-2xl bg-warning-subtle text-warning-amber flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
          <Bus className="w-6 h-6" />
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-ink-bright">No Coaches Scheduled</h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-ink-secondary mt-1 max-w-md mx-auto">
          No published schedules matched this exact route and date. Try selecting another date or searching for neighboring major hubs.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 px-1">
        <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-ink-secondary">
          Found <span className="font-bold text-ink-bright">{schedules.length}</span> available coaches
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs text-ink-muted font-medium flex items-center gap-1">
            <ArrowUpDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Sort by:
          </span>
          <div className="inline-flex rounded-xl bg-surface-card border border-border-strong p-0.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSortChange('departureTime')}
              className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all ${
                sortBy === 'departureTime'
                  ? 'bg-brand-primary text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-ink-secondary hover:text-ink-primary'
              }`}
            >
              Departure
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSortChange('fare')}
              className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all ${
                sortBy === 'fare'
                  ? 'bg-brand-primary text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-ink-secondary hover:text-ink-primary'
              }`}
            >
              Price
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4">
        {schedules.map((schedule) => (
          <ScheduleCard key={schedule.scheduleId} schedule={schedule} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\seats\types.ts
// ============================================================================
/**
 * Seat Matrix & Availability Types
 * Contract Reference: 07-API-AND-REALTIME-CONTRACTS.md Section 4.3 & 4.4
 */

export type SeatState = 'AVAILABLE' | 'SELECTED' | 'LOCKED' | 'BOOKED' | 'FEMALE_ONLY';
export type SeatClass = 'SEATER' | 'SLEEPER';

export interface Seat {
  id: string;
  seatLabel: string;
  seatRow: string | number;
  seatColumn: number;
  seatType: SeatClass;
  isFemaleOnly: boolean;
  status: 'AVAILABLE' | 'LOCKED' | 'BOOKED' | 'BLOCKED';
  lockedUntil?: string | null;
}

export interface SeatLayout {
  rowsCount: number;
  columnsCount: number;
  aisleCells: Array<{ row: string | number; column: number }>;
}

export interface ScheduleDetail {
  scheduleId: string;
  status: string;
  busType: string;
  operatorName: string;
  departureTime: string;
  arrivalTime: string;
  durationMinutes: number;
  fare: { amountPaise: number; currency: string } | number;
  availableSeats: number;
  bus: {
    id: string;
    registrationNumber: string;
    model: string;
    capacity: number;
    busType: string;
    amenities: string[];
  };
  route: {
    id: string;
    originCity: { id: string; name: string; state: string };
    destinationCity: { id: string; name: string; state: string };
    stands: Array<{
      stand: { id: string; cityId: string; name: string; type: string };
      sequence: number;
    }>;
  };
}

export interface SeatLockResponse {
  lockId: string;
  expiresAt: string;
  remainingSeconds: number;
}

export interface ActiveLock {
  lockId: string;
  seatId: string;
  seatLabel: string;
  scheduleId: string;
  expiresAt: string;
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\seats\api\seats.api.ts
// ============================================================================
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { ScheduleDetail, Seat, SeatLayout, SeatLockResponse } from '../types';

export async function fetchScheduleDetail(scheduleId: string): Promise<ScheduleDetail> {
  const response = await api.get<ScheduleDetail>(`/public/schedules/${scheduleId}`);
  return response.data;
}

export interface SeatAvailabilityResult {
  seats: Seat[];
  layout: SeatLayout;
}

function parseBackendSeat(raw: any, index: number): Seat {
  const id = raw.id || raw.seatId;
  const label = raw.label || raw.seatLabel || `S${index + 1}`;
  const row = String(raw.row || raw.seatRow || label.charAt(0) || 'A');
  const column = Number(raw.column || raw.seatColumn || label.slice(1) || (index % 4) + 1);

  // Correctly map backend status (which comes as raw.status, not raw.state)
  let status: 'AVAILABLE' | 'LOCKED' | 'BOOKED' | 'BLOCKED' = 'AVAILABLE';
  if (raw.status === 'LOCKED') status = 'LOCKED';
  else if (raw.status === 'BOOKED') status = 'BOOKED';
  else if (raw.status === 'BLOCKED') status = 'BLOCKED';

  return {
    id,
    seatLabel: label,
    seatRow: row,
    seatColumn: column,
    seatType: raw.type || raw.seatType || 'SEATER',
    isFemaleOnly: Boolean(raw.isFemaleOnly),
    status,
    lockedUntil: raw.lockedUntil || null,
  };
}

export async function fetchSeatAvailability(scheduleId: string, capacity: number = 40): Promise<SeatAvailabilityResult> {
  const res = await api.get<any>(`/passenger/schedules/${scheduleId}/availability`);
  const rawList = res.data?.seats || (Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : []);

  // If the backend returns no seats, it means the bus layout is missing OR the bus is PENDING.
  // Do NOT generate fake seats. Throw an error so the UI can show a proper message.
  if (!rawList || rawList.length === 0) {
    throw new Error("Seat layout not available. The bus may be pending approval.");
  }

  const seats = rawList.map((s: any, idx: number) => parseBackendSeat(s, idx));
  const backendLayout = res.data?.layout;

  const layout: SeatLayout = {
    rowsCount: backendLayout?.rowsCount || Math.ceil(seats.length / 4),
    columnsCount: backendLayout?.columnsCount || 4,
    aisleCells: backendLayout?.aisleCells || [],
  };

  return { seats, layout };
}

export async function lockSeat(scheduleId: string, seatId: string): Promise<SeatLockResponse> {
  const response = await api.post<SeatLockResponse>('/passenger/seat-locks', {
    scheduleId,
    seatId,
  });
  return response.data;
}

export async function releaseSeatLock(lockId: string): Promise<void> {
  await api.delete(`/passenger/seat-locks/${lockId}`);
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\seats\components\LockCountdown.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { formatCountdown } from '@/lib/utils/formatters';
import { Clock, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';

interface LockCountdownProps {
  expiresAt: string; // ISO 8601 UTC
  onExpire: () => void;
}

export function LockCountdown({ expiresAt, onExpire }: LockCountdownProps) {
  const [remainingSeconds, setRemainingSeconds] = useState<number>(() => {
    return Math.max(0, Math.floor((new Date(expiresAt).getTime() - Date.now()) / 1000));
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      const diff = Math.max(0, Math.floor((new Date(expiresAt).getTime() - Date.now()) / 1000));
      setRemainingSeconds(diff);

      if (diff <= 0) {
        clearInterval(timer);
        onExpire();
      }
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [expiresAt, onExpire]);

  const isCritical = remainingSeconds <= 60;

  return (
    <div
      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-mono font-bold transition-all ${
        isCritical
          ? 'bg-danger-subtle border-danger-crimson text-danger-crimson animate-pulse'
          : 'bg-warning-subtle border-warning-amber text-warning-amber'
      }`}
    >
      {isCritical ? <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
      <span>Seat Locked: {formatCountdown(remainingSeconds)}</span>
    </div>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\seats\components\SeatCell.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { Seat } from '../types';

interface SeatCellProps {
  seat: Seat;
  isSelected: boolean;
  onSelect: (seat: Seat) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function SeatCell({ seat, isSelected, onSelect, disabled = false }: SeatCellProps) {
  const isBooked = seat.status === 'BOOKED' || seat.status === 'BLOCKED';
  const isLocked = seat.status === 'LOCKED' && !isSelected;
  const isFemale = seat.isFemaleOnly && !isSelected && !isBooked && !isLocked;

  let bg = 'var(--seat-available-bg)';
  let border = 'var(--seat-available-border)';
  let color = 'var(--seat-available-text)';
  let extraClass = 'hover:-translate-y-0.5 cursor-pointer shadow-sm';

  if (isSelected) {
    bg = 'var(--seat-selected-bg)';
    border = 'var(--seat-selected-border)';
    color = 'var(--seat-selected-text)';
    extraClass = 'shadow-glow -translate-y-0.5 scale-105';
  } else if (isLocked) {
    bg = 'var(--seat-locked-bg)';
    border = 'var(--seat-locked-border)';
    color = 'var(--seat-locked-text)';
    extraClass = 'animate-seat-lock cursor-not-allowed';
  } else if (isBooked) {
    bg = 'var(--seat-booked-bg)';
    border = 'var(--seat-booked-border)';
    color = 'var(--seat-booked-text)';
    extraClass = 'cursor-not-allowed opacity-75';
  } else if (isFemale) {
    bg = 'var(--seat-female-bg)';
    border = 'var(--seat-female-border)';
    color = 'var(--seat-female-text)';
  }

  const handleClick = () => {
    if (disabled || isBooked || isLocked) return;
    onSelect(seat);
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleClick}
      disabled={disabled || isBooked || isLocked}
      aria-label={`Seat ${seat.seatLabel} ${isSelected ? 'Selected' : seat.status}`}
      className={`relative w-11 h-12 rounded-xl border-2 flex flex-col items-center justify-center font-mono text-xs font-bold transition-all duration-200 select-none ${extraClass}`}
      style={{
        backgroundColor: bg,
        borderColor: border,
        color: color,
      }}
    >
      <span>{seat.seatLabel}</span>
      {/* Visual seat cushion accent */}
      <span
        className="w-6 h-1 rounded-full opacity-40 mt-0.5"
        style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
      />
    </button>
  );
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\seats\components\SeatDeck.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { Seat, SeatLayout } from '../types';
import { SeatCell } from './SeatCell';
import { Disc3 } from 'lucide-react';

interface SeatDeckProps {
  seats: Seat[];
  layout: SeatLayout;
  selectedSeatId: string | null;
  onSeatClick: (seat: Seat) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function SeatDeck({
  seats,
  layout,
  selectedSeatId,
  onSeatClick,
  disabled = false,
}: SeatDeckProps) {
  // Group seats by their distinct row
  const rowMap = new Map<string, Seat[]>();
  seats.forEach((seat) => {
    const rowKey = String(seat.seatRow);
    if (!rowMap.has(rowKey)) {
      rowMap.set(rowKey, []);
    }
    rowMap.get(rowKey)!.push(seat);
  });

  // Sort seats within each row by column ascending
  rowMap.forEach((rowSeats) => {
    rowSeats.sort((a, b) => a.seatColumn - b.seatColumn);
  });

  // Sort row entries (e.g. A, B, C or 1, 2, 3)
  const sortedRows = Array.from(rowMap.entries()).sort(([a], [b]) => {
    const numA = parseInt(a, 10);
    const numB = parseInt(b, 10);
    if (!isNaN(numA) && !isNaN(numB)) return numA - numB;
    return a.localeCompare(b);
  });

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl bg-surface-card border-2 border-border-strong shadow-floating relative">
      {/* Front Windshield & Steering Cab */}
      <div className="relative mb-6 pb-4 border-b-2 border-dashed border-border-subtle flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="h-6 px-3 rounded-t-lg bg-telemetry-subtle border border-telemetry-cyan flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-telemetry-cyan uppercase tracking-wider">
          Windshield
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-ink-muted">
          <Disc3 className="w-6 h-6 animate-spin text-ink-secondary" style={{ animationDuration: '8s' }} />
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold uppercase">Driver</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Seat Rows (Vertical coach layout) */}
      <div className="space-y-3.5">
        {sortedRows.map(([rowKey, rowSeats]) => {
          // 2x2 layout separation: Left pair (columns <= 2) vs Right pair (columns > 2)
          const leftPair = rowSeats.filter((s) => s.seatColumn <= 2);
          const rightPair = rowSeats.filter((s) => s.seatColumn > 2);

          return (
            <div key={rowKey} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              {/* Left Seat Pair */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {leftPair.map((seat) => (
                  <SeatCell
                    key={seat.id}
                    seat={seat}
                    isSelected={selectedSeatId === seat.id}
                    onSelect={onSeatClick}
                    disabled={disabled}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* Central Walking Aisle */}
              <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-mono font-bold text-ink-muted/50 select-none">
                  {rowKey}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Right Seat Pair */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {rightPair.map((seat) => (
                  <SeatCell
                    key={seat.id}
                    seat={seat}
                    isSelected={selectedSeatId === seat.id}
                    onSelect={onSeatClick}
                    disabled={disabled}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Rear Coach Exit */}
      <div className="mt-8 pt-3 border-t-2 border-border-subtle text-center">
        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-ink-muted uppercase tracking-widest">
          Coach Rear Entry
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\telemetry\components\LiveMap.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { MapContainer, TileLayer, Marker, Popup, useMap } from 'react-leaflet';
import 'leaflet/dist/leaflet.css';
import L from 'leaflet';
import { LiveGpsTelemetry, StopEta } from '@/features/tickets/types';

// Fix for missing default marker icons in Next.js (Webpack doesn't bundle Leaflet's images)
const customIcon = new L.DivIcon({
  className: 'custom-div-icon',
  html: `<div style="background-color:var(--brand-primary);width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:50%;border:3px solid white;box-shadow:0 0 12px rgba(0, 85, 237, 0.8);"></div>`,
  iconSize: [24, 24],
  iconAnchor: [12, 12],
});

// Component to handle smooth panning when GPS updates
function MapUpdater({ center }: { center: [number, number] }) {
  const map = useMap();
  useEffect(() => {
    map.setView(center, map.getZoom(), { animate: true });
  }, [center, map]);
  return null;
}

interface LiveMapProps {
  gps?: LiveGpsTelemetry;
  stops: StopEta[];
}

export function LiveMap({ gps, stops }: LiveMapProps) {
  // Default to center of India if no GPS
  const defaultCenter: [number, number] = [20.5937, 78.9629];
  const center: [number, number] = gps ? [gps.lat, gps.lng] : defaultCenter;

  return (
    <div className="h-[400px] w-full rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border-2 border-border-hairline relative z-0 my-6 shadow-inner">
      <MapContainer center={center} zoom={gps ? 13 : 5} style={{ height: '100%', width: '100%' }} scrollWheelZoom={false}>
        <TileLayer
          attribution='&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a>'
          url="https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png"
        />
        {gps && (
          <>
            <MapUpdater center={[gps.lat, gps.lng]} />
            <Marker position={[gps.lat, gps.lng]} icon={customIcon}>
              <Popup>
                <div className="text-xs font-sans">
                  <strong className="block text-sm mb-1">Live Vehicle</strong>
                  Speed: {gps.speedKmh} km/h<br/>
                  Updated: {new Date(gps.recordedAt).toLocaleTimeString()}
                </div>
              </Popup>
            </Marker>
          </>
        )}
      </MapContainer>
      
      {!gps && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40 z-[1000] flex items-center justify-center pointer-events-none">
          <span className="bg-surface-card px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold text-ink-bright shadow-floating">
            Waiting for GPS Signal...
          </span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\telemetry\components\LiveTelemetryHud.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { LiveTripSnapshot } from '@/features/tickets/types';
import { formatISTTime } from '@/lib/utils/formatters';
import { LiveMap } from './LiveMap';
import { Radio, Bus, MapPin, Gauge, Clock, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';

interface LiveTelemetryHudProps {
  snapshot: LiveTripSnapshot;
}

export function LiveTelemetryHud({ snapshot }: LiveTelemetryHudProps) {
  const isEnRoute = snapshot.status === 'IN_PROGRESS';
  const speed = snapshot.gps?.speedKmh || 0;

  const stops = snapshot.stops || [];
  const passedStops = stops.filter((s) => s.status === 'PASSED').length;
  const progressPct = stops.length > 1 ? Math.min(100, Math.round((passedStops / (stops.length - 1)) * 100)) : 45;

  const nextStop = stops.find((s) => s.status === 'PENDING') || stops[stops.length - 1];

  return (
    <div className="p-6 md:p-8 rounded-3xl bg-surface-card border-2 border-border-strong shadow-floating">
      {/* Top Beacon Status Bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-6 border-b border-border-hairline">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
              {snapshot.bus?.model} ({snapshot.bus?.registrationNumber})
            </h2>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-surface-sunken border border-border-subtle uppercase">
              {snapshot.bus?.busType}
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs text-ink-muted">TRIP ID: {snapshot.tripId.slice(0, 8)}...</span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {isEnRoute ? (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-signal-subtle border border-signal-emerald text-signal-emerald text-xs font-mono font-bold">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-signal-emerald animate-ping" />
              GPS STREAM ACTIVE
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-surface-sunken border border-border-subtle text-ink-muted text-xs font-mono font-semibold">
              <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              {snapshot.status}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Real Map Tiles */}
      <LiveMap gps={snapshot.gps} stops={stops} />

      {/* Progress Track with Moving Vehicle Marker */}
      <div className="my-8 px-4">
        <div className="relative h-2.5 bg-surface-sunken rounded-full">
          <div
            className="absolute top-0 left-0 h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-1000 shadow-glow"
            style={{
              width: `${progressPct}%`,
              background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, var(--brand-primary), var(--telemetry-cyan))',
            }}
          />

          <div
            className="absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-surface-card border-2 border-telemetry-cyan flex items-center justify-center shadow-glow transition-all duration-1000"
            style={{ left: `${progressPct}%` }}
          >
            <Bus className="w-4 h-4 text-telemetry-cyan" />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-[10px] font-mono text-ink-muted mt-3">
          <span>{stops[0]?.name || 'Origin Stand'}</span>
          <span>{stops[stops.length - 1]?.name || 'Final Stand'}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Telemetry Metrics Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-canvas-subtle border border-border-hairline">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-ink-muted text-xs font-mono mb-1">
            <span>VELOCITY</span>
            <Gauge className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-telemetry-cyan" />
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-black text-ink-bright">{speed} km/h</div>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-signal-emerald font-semibold">
            {speed > 0 ? 'Normal Highway Speed' : 'Stationary at Stop'}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-canvas-subtle border border-border-hairline">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-ink-muted text-xs font-mono mb-1">
            <span>NEXT STAND</span>
            <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-brand-primary" />
          </div>
          <div className="text-base font-extrabold text-ink-bright truncate">
            {nextStop?.name || 'In Transit'}
          </div>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-telemetry-cyan font-semibold">
            ETA: {nextStop?.estimatedArrival ? formatISTTime(nextStop.estimatedArrival) : 'Calculating...'}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-canvas-subtle border border-border-hairline">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-ink-muted text-xs font-mono mb-1">
            <span>SCHEDULE STATUS</span>
            <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-warning-amber" />
          </div>
          <div className="text-base font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
            {snapshot.delayMinutes > 0 ? `+${snapshot.delayMinutes} min delay` : 'On Time'}
          </div>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-ink-muted">Synchronized via Socket.io</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Ticket, Refund Preview & Telemetry Types
 * Contract Reference: 07-API-AND-REALTIME-CONTRACTS.md Section 4.5, 4.7
 */

export interface TicketData {
  ticketId: string;
  bookingId: string;
  qrPayload: string; // Wire format: v1:{base64}:{base64}
  status: 'ACTIVE' | 'BOARDED' | 'CANCELLED';
  issuedAt: string;
}

export type RefundTier = 'TIER_FULL' | 'TIER_75' | 'TIER_50' | 'TIER_ZERO' | 'TIER_NOT_ELIGIBLE';

export interface RefundPreviewData {
  bookingId: string;
  tier: RefundTier;
  amountPaise: number;
  cancellationFeePaise: number;
  departureTime: string;
  hoursToDeparture: number;
}

export interface LiveGpsTelemetry {
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  speedKmh: number;
  recordedAt: string;
}

export interface StopEta {
  standId: string;
  name: string;
  type: string;
  sequence: number;
  scheduledArrival: string;
  estimatedArrival: string;
  status: 'PENDING' | 'ARRIVED' | 'PASSED';
}

export interface LiveTripSnapshot {
  tripId: string;
  scheduleId: string;
  status: 'SCHEDULED' | 'IN_PROGRESS' | 'COMPLETED' | 'CANCELLED';
  delayMinutes: number;
  bus: {
    registrationNumber: string;
    model: string;
    busType: string;
  };
  gps?: LiveGpsTelemetry;
  stops: StopEta[];
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\tickets\api\tickets.api.ts
// ============================================================================
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { TicketData, RefundPreviewData, LiveTripSnapshot } from '../types';
import { BookingResponse } from '@/features/booking/types';

export async function fetchBookingTicket(bookingId: string): Promise<TicketData> {
  const response = await api.get<TicketData>(`/passenger/bookings/${bookingId}/ticket`);
  return response.data;
}

export async function fetchRefundPreview(bookingId: string): Promise<RefundPreviewData> {
  const response = await api.get<RefundPreviewData>(`/passenger/bookings/${bookingId}/refund-preview`);
  return response.data;
}

export async function executeCancelBooking(bookingId: string): Promise<{
  status: string;
  refund: {
    refundId: string;
    tier: string;
    amountPaise: number;
    status: string;
  };
}> {
  const response = await api.post<any>(`/passenger/bookings/${bookingId}/cancel`, {});
  return response.data;
}

export async function fetchPassengerBookings(limit = 20, cursor?: string | null): Promise<{
  data: BookingResponse[];
  nextCursor?: string | null;
}> {
  const url = `/passenger/bookings?limit=${limit}${cursor ? `&cursor=${cursor}` : ''}`;
  const response = await api.get<BookingResponse[]>(url);
  return {
    data: response.data || [],
    nextCursor: response.meta?.nextCursor,
  };
}

export async function fetchLiveTripSnapshot(tripId: string): Promise<LiveTripSnapshot> {
  const response = await api.get<LiveTripSnapshot>(`/passenger/trips/${tripId}/live`);
  return response.data;
}


// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\features\tickets\components\CancelModal.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { RefundPreviewData } from '../types';
import { executeCancelBooking } from '../api/tickets.api';
import { formatINR } from '@/lib/utils/formatters';
import { AlertCircle, AlertTriangle, Loader2, X, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';

interface CancelModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  bookingId: string;
  preview: RefundPreviewData | null;
  onCancelled: () => void;
}

const REFUND_TIER_EXPLANATIONS: Record<string, string> = {
  TIER_FULL: 'Full 100% refund (cancelled more than 24h prior to departure or operator action).',
  TIER_75: '75% refund tier (cancelled between 12h and 24h prior to departure).',
  TIER_50: '50% refund tier (cancelled between 2h and 12h prior to departure).',
  TIER_ZERO: 'Zero refund (cancelled within 2 hours of departure window).',
  TIER_NOT_ELIGIBLE: 'Booking is not eligible for cancellation refund.',
};

export function CancelModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  bookingId,
  preview,
  onCancelled,
}: CancelModalProps) {
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleConfirmCancel = async () => {
    setSubmitting(true);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    try {
      await executeCancelBooking(bookingId);
      onCancelled();
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message || 'Cancellation could not be completed.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-canvas/80 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md p-6 rounded-3xl bg-surface-card border border-border-strong shadow-modal relative">
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-5 right-5 p-1 rounded-full text-ink-muted hover:text-ink-primary hover:bg-surface-hover"
        >
          <X className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-danger-subtle text-danger-crimson flex items-center justify-center">
            <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-ink-bright">Cancel Reservation?</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-secondary">Refund calculation per official transit rules</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {errorMsg && (
          <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-danger-subtle text-danger-crimson text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span>{errorMsg}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {preview && (
          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-canvas-subtle border border-border-subtle space-y-3 mb-6">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="text-ink-secondary">Refund Tier:</span>
              <span className="font-mono font-bold text-ink-bright px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-surface-card border border-border-subtle">
                {preview.tier}
              </span>
            </div>

            <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-muted leading-relaxed">
              {REFUND_TIER_EXPLANATIONS[preview.tier] || 'Standard policy applies.'}
            </p>

            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-border-hairline flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-bright">Refundable Amount:</span>
              <span className="text-lg font-extrabold text-signal-emerald">
                {formatINR(preview.amountPaise)}
              </span>
            </div>

            {preview.cancellationFeePaise > 0 && (
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-ink-muted">
                <span>Cancellation Fee:</span>
                <span>{formatINR(preview.cancellationFeePaise)}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-1/2 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl border border-border-strong text-ink-secondary hover:bg-surface-hover transition-colors"
          >
            Keep Reservation
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleConfirmCancel}
            disabled={submitting}
            className="w-1/2 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl bg-danger-crimson text-white hover:opacity-90 shadow-glow disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all cursor-pointer"
          >
            {submitting ? (
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
            ) : (
              <span>Confirm Cancel</span>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
