'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { QRCodeSVG } from 'qrcode.react';
import { TicketData } from '../types';
import { BookingResponse } from '@/features/booking/types';
import { formatISTTime, formatISTDate } from '@/lib/utils/formatters';
import { ArrowRight, ShieldCheck, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';

interface TicketStubProps {
  booking: BookingResponse;
  ticket: TicketData;
}

/**
 * Dual-Mode Cryptographic Boarding Pass
 * Specified in Design_guide.html Section 7
 * Isolates raw Ed25519 QR wire payload on an optical high-contrast island
 */
export function TicketStub({ booking, ticket }: TicketStubProps) {
  const isBoarded = ticket.status === 'BOARDED';
  const isCancelled = booking.status === 'CANCELLED' || ticket.status === 'CANCELLED';

  const originName = booking.schedule?.route?.originCity?.name || booking.boardingStand?.name || 'Origin';
  const destName = booking.schedule?.route?.destinationCity?.name || booking.droppingStand?.name || 'Destination';

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto rounded-3xl bg-surface-card border-2 border-border-strong shadow-floating overflow-hidden relative">
      {/* Upper Ticket Section */}
      <div className="p-6 sm:p-8 pb-10 border-b-2 border-dashed border-border-strong relative">
        {/* Scalloped Side Cutouts */}
        <div className="absolute -bottom-4 -left-4 w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-canvas border-r-2 border-border-strong" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-4 -right-4 w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-canvas border-l-2 border-border-strong" />

        {/* Status Ribbon */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold tracking-wider uppercase px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-brand-subtle text-brand-primary border border-brand-border">
            Verified Pass
          </span>

          {isBoarded ? (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-signal-emerald px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-signal-subtle border border-signal-emerald">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Boarded
            </span>
          ) : isCancelled ? (
            <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-danger-crimson px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-danger-subtle border border-danger-crimson">
              Cancelled
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-[11px] font-mono font-bold text-signal-emerald flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-signal-emerald animate-pulse" />
              Confirmed
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Route City Pair */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 my-2">
          <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-ink-bright tracking-tight">
            {originName.toUpperCase()}
          </div>
          <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5 text-telemetry-cyan flex-shrink-0" />
          <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-ink-bright tracking-tight text-right">
            {destName.toUpperCase()}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="text-xs text-ink-muted">
          {booking.boardingStand?.name} → {booking.droppingStand?.name}
        </div>

        {/* Departure Details Grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-border-hairline text-xs">
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-ink-muted block">Departure</span>
            <span className="font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
              {booking.schedule?.departureTime ? formatISTTime(booking.schedule.departureTime) : '--:--'}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-ink-secondary block">
              {booking.schedule?.departureTime ? formatISTDate(booking.schedule.departureTime) : ''}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="text-center">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-ink-muted block">Seat Alloc</span>
            <span className="font-extrabold text-brand-primary text-sm">
              {booking.passengers?.map((p) => p.seatLabel).join(', ') || 'Assigned'}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="text-right">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-ink-muted block">Coach Class</span>
            <span className="font-extrabold text-ink-bright">
              {booking.schedule?.busType || 'AC Executive'}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Lower Stub with Optical White QR Island */}
      <div className="p-6 bg-surface-hover flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="text-sm font-bold text-ink-bright">
            {booking.passengers?.[0]?.name || 'Passenger'}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs font-mono text-ink-muted mt-0.5">
            PNR: <span className="text-ink-bright font-bold">{booking.bookingReference}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-signal-emerald font-semibold mt-2">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Ed25519 Signed</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Optical Scan Island: Pure white box preserves camera readability in dark mode */}
        <div className="w-[104px] h-[104px] bg-white p-2 rounded-2xl border border-border-subtle shadow-ambient flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
          {ticket.qrPayload ? (
            <QRCodeSVG
              value={ticket.qrPayload}
              size={88}
              level="M"
              bgColor="#FFFFFF"
              fgColor="#000000"
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-ink-muted text-center">QR NOT ISSUED</div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
export const CONFIG = {
  API_BASE: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE || 'http://localhost:4310/v1',
  WS_URL: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WS_URL || 'http://localhost:4310',
  RAZORPAY_KEY_ID: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RAZORPAY_KEY_ID || '',
  IS_DEV: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_DEV_MODE === 'true',
  BOOKING_EXPIRY_MINUTES: 15,
  SEAT_LOCK_TTL_SECONDS: 300,
} as const;



// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\lib\api\client.ts
// ============================================================================
import { ApiResponse, ApiError, ApiErrorResponse } from './types';
import { CONFIG } from '../config';

type HttpMethod = 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE';

interface RequestOptions {
  method?: HttpMethod;
  body?: unknown;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  idempotencyKey?: string;
  skipAuth?: boolean;
}

// Global refresh queue to avoid token refresh races across concurrent calls
let isRefreshing = false;
let refreshSubscribers: Array<(token: string) => void> = [];

function onTokenRefreshed(token: string) {
  refreshSubscribers.forEach((callback) => callback(token));
  refreshSubscribers = [];
}

function addRefreshSubscriber(callback: (token: string) => void) {
  refreshSubscribers.push(callback);
}

export class HttpClient {
  private baseUrl: string;

  constructor() {
    // In browser: use relative '/api/v1' to hit Next.js rewrite proxy (zero CORS drift)
    // Server-side: use absolute backend URL from config
    this.baseUrl = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? '/api/v1' : CONFIG.API_BASE;
  }

  private getAccessToken(): string | null {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
    return localStorage.getItem('busverse_access_token');
  }

  private getRefreshToken(): string | null {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
    return localStorage.getItem('busverse_refresh_token');
  }

  private setTokens(accessToken: string, refreshToken: string) {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
    localStorage.setItem('busverse_access_token', accessToken);
    localStorage.setItem('busverse_refresh_token', refreshToken);
  }

  private clearAuthAndLogout() {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
    localStorage.removeItem('busverse_access_token');
    localStorage.removeItem('busverse_refresh_token');
    localStorage.removeItem('busverse_user');
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('busverse:auth:logout'));
  }

  public async request<T>(endpoint: string, options: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<ApiResponse<T>> {
    const { method = 'GET', body, headers = {}, idempotencyKey, skipAuth = false } = options;

    const targetUrl = endpoint.startsWith('http')
      ? endpoint
      : `${this.baseUrl}${endpoint.startsWith('/') ? endpoint : `/${endpoint}`}`;

    const requestHeaders: Record<string, string> = {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'X-Request-Id': `req_${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 12)}`,
      ...headers,
    };

    if (idempotencyKey) {
      requestHeaders['Idempotency-Key'] = idempotencyKey;
    }

    if (!skipAuth) {
      const token = this.getAccessToken();
      if (token) {
        requestHeaders['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
      }
    }

    const config: RequestInit = {
      method,
      headers: requestHeaders,
    };

    if (body !== undefined && method !== 'GET') {
      config.body = JSON.stringify(body);
    }

    const response = await fetch(targetUrl, config);

    // 401 Unauthorized handling: Auto-rotate access token and replay
    if (response.status === 401 && !skipAuth) {
      const refreshToken = this.getRefreshToken();
      if (!refreshToken) {
        this.clearAuthAndLogout();
        const errJson = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as ApiErrorResponse;
        throw new ApiError(401, errJson.error || { code: 'TOKEN_EXPIRED', message: 'Session expired' });
      }

      if (!isRefreshing) {
        isRefreshing = true;

        this.performTokenRefresh(refreshToken)
          .then((newAccessToken) => {
            isRefreshing = false;
            onTokenRefreshed(newAccessToken);
          })
          .catch((refreshErr) => {
            isRefreshing = false;
            refreshSubscribers = [];
            this.clearAuthAndLogout();
            throw refreshErr;
          });
      }

      // Wait for active refresh to resolve, then replay original request
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        addRefreshSubscriber(async (newAccessToken: string) => {
          try {
            requestHeaders['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${newAccessToken}`;
            const replayed = await fetch(targetUrl, { ...config, headers: requestHeaders });
            const data = await this.handleResponse<T>(replayed);
            resolve(data);
          } catch (replayErr) {
            reject(replayErr);
          }
        });
      });
    }

    // 403 Forbidden: NON-NEGOTIABLE RULE 8.3 — NEVER attempt refresh on 403 (role mismatch)
    return this.handleResponse<T>(response);
  }

  private async performTokenRefresh(refreshToken: string): Promise<string> {
    const refreshUrl = `${this.baseUrl}/passenger/auth/refresh`;
    const res = await fetch(refreshUrl, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ refreshToken }),
    });

    if (!res.ok) {
      this.clearAuthAndLogout();
      throw new ApiError(res.status, {
        code: 'SESSION_REVOKED',
        message: 'Your credentials could not be rotated. Please log in again.',
      });
    }

    const payload = (await res.json()) as ApiResponse<{
      accessToken: string;
      refreshToken: string;
    }>;

    this.setTokens(payload.data.accessToken, payload.data.refreshToken);
    return payload.data.accessToken;
  }

  private async handleResponse<T>(response: Response): Promise<ApiResponse<T>> {
    const contentType = response.headers.get('content-type');
    const isJson = contentType && contentType.includes('application/json');

    if (!response.ok) {
      if (isJson) {
        const errorJson = (await response.json()) as ApiErrorResponse;
        throw new ApiError(
          response.status,
          errorJson.error || {
            code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR',
            message: `Request failed with status ${response.status}`,
          }
        );
      }
      throw new ApiError(response.status, {
        code: 'NETWORK_ERROR',
        message: response.statusText || 'Network request failed',
      });
    }

    if (response.status === 204) {
      return { data: {} as T };
    }

    return (await response.json()) as ApiResponse<T>;
  }

  // Convenience verbs
  public get<T>(endpoint: string, headers?: Record<string, string>) {
    return this.request<T>(endpoint, { method: 'GET', headers });
  }

  public post<T>(endpoint: string, body?: unknown, options: Omit<RequestOptions, 'method' | 'body'> = {}) {
    return this.request<T>(endpoint, { method: 'POST', body, ...options });
  }

  public patch<T>(endpoint: string, body?: unknown, headers?: Record<string, string>) {
    return this.request<T>(endpoint, { method: 'PATCH', body, headers });
  }

  public delete<T>(endpoint: string, headers?: Record<string, string>) {
    return this.request<T>(endpoint, { method: 'DELETE', headers });
  }
}

export const api = new HttpClient();

/**
 * Canonical 52 Error Codes & User-Facing Explanations

export const ERROR_MESSAGES: Record<string, string> = {

  INVALID_PHONE_FORMAT: 'Please enter a valid 10-digit mobile number with country code (+91).',
  INVALID_EMAIL_FORMAT: 'Please provide a valid email format.',
  INVALID_CREDENTIALS: 'The login credentials entered do not match our records.',
  EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED: 'Please verify your email address to proceed.',
  SESSION_REVOKED: 'Your session has been terminated. Please log in again.',
  TOKEN_EXPIRED: 'Session expired. Automatically refreshing your credentials...',
  TOKEN_INVALID: 'Invalid authentication token. Please log in again.',
  EMAIL_SIGNUP_NOT_SUPPORTED: 'Signup requires a mobile number. Email login is supported for existing users.',

  // Seat Lock & Concurrency errors
  SEAT_LOCKED: 'This seat is currently held by another passenger. Please pick another seat.',
  SEAT_ALREADY_BOOKED: 'This seat was just confirmed by another passenger.',
  SEAT_LOCK_EXPIRED: 'Your 5-minute seat lock reservation timed out. Please select again.',
  LOCK_COUNT_EXCEEDED: 'Maximum seat allocation limit reached for this booking.',
  SEAT_NOT_AVAILABLE: 'This seat is not currently open for reservations.',

  BOOKING_NOT_CANCELLABLE: 'This trip cannot be cancelled at this stage.',

  SCHEDULE_NOT_PUBLISHED: 'This transit route is not currently taking passenger reservations.',

  // Rate Limiting & Networking
  RATE_LIMITED: 'You are making requests too quickly. Please wait a moment and retry.',

  SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE: 'Transit service temporarily degraded. Please try again in a few moments.',

  VALIDATION_ERROR: 'Submitted information failed verification. Please review highlighted fields.'
};

export function getErrorMessage(code: string, fallback?: string): string {
  return ERROR_MESSAGES[code] || fallback || 'An unexpected transit error occurred. Please try again.';
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\lib\api\types.ts
// ============================================================================
/**
 * BusVerse API Response Envelopes
 * Strict conformity to 07-API-AND-REALTIME-CONTRACTS.md v1.4
 */

export interface ApiResponse<T> {
  data: T;
  meta?: {
    requestId?: string;
    durationMs?: number;
    nextCursor?: string | null;
    note?: string;
  };
}

export interface ApiErrorDetail {
  code: string;
  message: string;
  details?: Record<string, unknown> | Array<unknown>;
  requestId?: string;
}

export interface ApiErrorResponse {
  error: ApiErrorDetail;
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  public readonly code: string;
  public readonly status: number;
  public readonly details?: Record<string, unknown> | Array<unknown>;
  public readonly requestId?: string;

  constructor(status: number, errorDetail: ApiErrorDetail) {
    super(errorDetail.message);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
    this.status = status;
    this.code = errorDetail.code;
    this.details = errorDetail.details;
    this.requestId = errorDetail.requestId;
    Object.setPrototypeOf(this, ApiError.prototype);
  }
}

// User & Session types
export interface UserSession {
  id: string;
  phone?: string;
  email?: string;
  name?: string;
  emailVerified?: boolean;
}

export interface AuthTokens {
  accessToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
  expiresAt: string;
  user: UserSession;
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\lib\auth\AuthContext.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { wsManager } from '../ws/socket';
import { UserSession } from '../api/types';

interface AuthContextType {
  user: UserSession | null;
  accessToken: string | null;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  isLoading: boolean;
  requestOtp: (phone: string, intent?: 'SIGNUP' | 'LOGIN') => Promise<{ expiresInSeconds: number }>;
  verifyOtp: (phone: string, otp: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<UserSession | null>(null);
  const [accessToken, setAccessToken] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  // Synchronize authentication tokens & user state from localStorage on client load
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const storedToken = localStorage.getItem('busverse_access_token');
      const storedUser = localStorage.getItem('busverse_user');

      if (storedToken && storedUser) {
        setAccessToken(storedToken);
        setUser(JSON.parse(storedUser));
        wsManager.connect(storedToken);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to parse local authentication cache:', e);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }

    const handleLogoutEvent = () => {
      setUser(null);
      setAccessToken(null);
      wsManager.disconnect();
    };

    window.addEventListener('busverse:auth:logout', handleLogoutEvent);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('busverse:auth:logout', handleLogoutEvent);
  }, []);

  const requestOtp = useCallback(async (phone: string, intent: 'SIGNUP' | 'LOGIN' = 'LOGIN') => {
    const res = await api.post<{ expiresInSeconds: number }>(
      '/passenger/auth/otp/request',
      { phone, intent },
      { skipAuth: true }
    );
    return res.data;
  }, []);

  const verifyOtp = useCallback(async (phone: string, otp: string) => {
    const res = await api.post<{
      accessToken: string;
      refreshToken: string;
      expiresAt: string;
      user: UserSession;
    }>(
      '/passenger/auth/otp/verify',
      { phone, otp },
      { skipAuth: true }
    );

    const { accessToken: newAccess, refreshToken: newRefresh, user: newUser } = res.data;

    localStorage.setItem('busverse_access_token', newAccess);
    localStorage.setItem('busverse_refresh_token', newRefresh);
    localStorage.setItem('busverse_user', JSON.stringify(newUser));

    setAccessToken(newAccess);
    setUser(newUser);
    wsManager.connect(newAccess);
  }, []);

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      if (accessToken) {
        await api.post('/passenger/auth/logout', {});
      }
    } catch {
      // Local clean-up proceeds even if server revoking network fails
    } finally {
      localStorage.removeItem('busverse_access_token');
      localStorage.removeItem('busverse_refresh_token');
      localStorage.removeItem('busverse_user');
      setUser(null);
      setAccessToken(null);
      wsManager.disconnect();
    }
  }, [accessToken]);

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        accessToken,
        isAuthenticated: !!user && !!accessToken,
        isLoading,
        requestOtp,
        verifyOtp,
        logout,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\lib\theme\ThemeProvider.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';

type Theme = 'light' | 'dark';

interface ThemeContextType {
  theme: Theme;
  toggleTheme: () => void;
  setTheme: (theme: Theme) => void;
}

const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [theme, setThemeState] = useState<Theme>('dark');

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('busverse_theme') as Theme | null;
    const initial = saved || 'dark';
    setThemeState(initial);
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', initial);
  }, []);

  const setTheme = (nextTheme: Theme) => {
    setThemeState(nextTheme);
    localStorage.setItem('busverse_theme', nextTheme);
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', nextTheme);
  };

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    const next = theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
    setTheme(next);
  };

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider value={{ theme, toggleTheme, setTheme }}>
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useTheme() {
  const context = useContext(ThemeContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useTheme must be used within a ThemeProvider');
  }
  return context;
}

'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useTheme } from './ThemeProvider';
import { Sun, Moon } from 'lucide-react';

export function ThemeToggle() {
  const { theme, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);

  if (!mounted) {
    return (
      <div className="w-[104px] h-[30px] rounded-full border border-border-strong bg-surface-card opacity-60" />
    );
  }

  return (
    <button
      onClick={toggleTheme}
      aria-label="Toggle visual theme"
      className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded-full border border-border-strong bg-surface-card hover:bg-surface-hover text-ink-primary transition-all duration-200 shadow-ambient"
    >
      {theme === 'dark' ? (
        <>
          <Sun className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-warning-amber" />
          <span>Light Mode</span>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <Moon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-brand-primary" />
          <span>Dark Mode</span>
        </>
      )}
    </button>
  );
}

// FILE: apps\busverse\src\lib\utils\formatters.ts
// ============================================================================
/**
 * BusVerse Transit Formatters
 * Strict adherence to IST timestamps and integer paise currency units
 */

// Formats integer paise to INR currency (e.g. 45000 -> ₹450)
export function formatINR(amountPaise: number): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-IN', {
    style: 'currency',
    currency: 'INR',
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(amountPaise / 100);
}

// Converts ISO UTC to Indian Standard Time (Asia/Kolkata)
export function formatISTTime(isoDateString: string): string {
  try {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-IN', {
      timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
      hour12: true,
    }).format(new Date(isoDateString));
  } catch {
    return '--:--';
  }
}

export function formatISTDate(isoDateString: string): string {
  try {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-IN', {
      timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata',
      weekday: 'short',
      day: 'numeric',
      month: 'short',
      year: 'numeric',
    }).format(new Date(isoDateString));
  } catch {
    return '--';
  }
}

// Formats duration in minutes to readable hours & minutes (e.g. 480 -> "8h 00m")
export function formatDuration(durationMinutes: number): string {
  const hours = Math.floor(durationMinutes / 60);
  const minutes = durationMinutes % 60;
  if (hours === 0) return `${minutes}m`;
  return `${hours}h ${minutes.toString().padStart(2, '0')}m`;
}

// Countdown formatter (seconds to MM:SS)
export function formatCountdown(remainingSeconds: number): string {
  const safe = Math.max(0, Math.floor(remainingSeconds));
  const m = Math.floor(safe / 60);
  const s = safe % 60;
  return `${m.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${s.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\lib\ws\socket.ts
// ============================================================================
import { io, Socket } from 'socket.io-client';
import { CONFIG } from '../config';

type SocketEventListener = (payload: any) => void;

class WebSocketManager {
  private socket: Socket | null = null;
  private subscribedTopics: Set<string> = new Set();
  private listeners: Map<string, Set<SocketEventListener>> = new Map();
  private authTimeoutTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;

  public connect(accessToken: string) {
    if (this.socket && this.socket.connected) {
      return;
    }

    if (this.socket) {
      this.socket.disconnect();
    }

    // Connect to backend Fastify Socket.io server
    this.socket = io(CONFIG.WS_URL, {
      path: '/v1/ws',
      transports: ['websocket', 'polling'],
      reconnectionDelay: 1000,
      reconnectionDelayMax: 16000,
      reconnectionAttempts: 5,
      autoConnect: true,
    });

    // RULE 8.11: Handshake timing — server terminates after 5s if auth is delayed
    this.socket.on('connect', () => {
      this.socket?.emit('message', {
        action: 'auth',
        token: accessToken,
      });

      // Clear safeguard timer once connect initiates
      if (this.authTimeoutTimer) clearTimeout(this.authTimeoutTimer);
    });

    this.socket.on('auth:ok', () => {
      // Re-subscribe to all active topics on reconnection
      if (this.subscribedTopics.size > 0) {
        this.socket?.emit('message', {
          action: 'subscribe',
          topics: Array.from(this.subscribedTopics),
        });
      }
    });

    // Server sends auth:expiring at (exp - 60s)
    this.socket.on('auth:expiring', async () => {
      const refreshToken = localStorage.getItem('busverse_refresh_token');
      if (refreshToken && this.socket) {
        this.socket.emit('message', {
          action: 'auth:refresh',
          refreshToken,
        });
      }
    });

    this.socket.on('auth:refreshed', (data: { accessToken: string; refreshToken: string }) => {
      localStorage.setItem('busverse_access_token', data.accessToken);
      localStorage.setItem('busverse_refresh_token', data.refreshToken);
    });

    // General message router for incoming events
    this.socket.on('message', (msg: { event?: string; topic?: string; data?: any }) => {
      if (msg.event) {
        const topicListeners = this.listeners.get(msg.event);
        if (topicListeners) {
          topicListeners.forEach((listener) => listener(msg.data));
        }
      }
    });

    // Close code management
    this.socket.on('disconnect', (reason) => {
      if (reason === 'io server disconnect') {
        // Closed by server (e.g., Code 4001, 4003 or 1008)
        const freshToken = localStorage.getItem('busverse_access_token');
        if (freshToken) {
          setTimeout(() => this.connect(freshToken), 1500);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  public subscribe(topics: string | string[]) {
    const topicArray = Array.isArray(topics) ? topics : [topics];
    topicArray.forEach((t) => this.subscribedTopics.add(t));

    if (this.socket?.connected) {
      this.socket.emit('message', {
        action: 'subscribe',
        topics: topicArray,
      });
    }
  }

  public unsubscribe(topics: string | string[]) {
    const topicArray = Array.isArray(topics) ? topics : [topics];
    topicArray.forEach((t) => this.subscribedTopics.delete(t));

    if (this.socket?.connected) {
      this.socket.emit('message', {
        action: 'unsubscribe',
        topics: topicArray,
      });
    }
  }

  public on(event: string, callback: SocketEventListener) {
    if (!this.listeners.has(event)) {
      this.listeners.set(event, new Set());
    }
    this.listeners.get(event)!.add(callback);

    // Also register on underlying socket instance
    this.socket?.on(event, callback);

    return () => {
      this.listeners.get(event)?.delete(callback);
      this.socket?.off(event, callback);
    };
  }

  public disconnect() {
    if (this.authTimeoutTimer) clearTimeout(this.authTimeoutTimer);
    this.subscribedTopics.clear();
    this.listeners.clear();
    this.socket?.disconnect();
    this.socket = null;
  }
}

export const wsManager = new WebSocketManager();



// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\lib\ws\useWsEvent.ts
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { wsManager } from './socket';

/**
 * useWsEvent hook
 * Subscribes to a WebSocket topic and attaches a listener with automatic cleanup.
 */
export function useWsEvent<T = any>(
  topic: string | null,
  event: string,
  handler: (payload: T) => void
) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!topic) return;

    wsManager.subscribe(topic);
    const unbind = wsManager.on(event, handler);

    return () => {
      unbind();
      wsManager.unsubscribe(topic);
    };
  }, [topic, event, handler]);
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: apps\busverse\src\providers\AppProviders.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { ThemeProvider } from '../lib/theme/ThemeProvider';
import { AuthProvider } from '../lib/auth/AuthContext';

export function AppProviders({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [queryClient] = useState(
    () =>
      new QueryClient({
        defaultOptions: {
          queries: {
            staleTime: 60 * 1000,
            retry: (failureCount, error: any) => {
              // Never retry on client authorization or 404 errors
              if (error?.status === 401 || error?.status === 403 || error?.status === 404) {
                return false;
              }
              return failureCount < 2;
            },
          },
        },
      })
  );

  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <ThemeProvider>
        <AuthProvider>
          {children}
        </AuthProvider>
      </ThemeProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}

   BUSVERSE DUAL-THEME ENGINE — DESIGN SPECIFICATION
   WCAG 2.2 Level AAA Verified Tokens for Light & Dark Spectrums

/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   LIGHT THEME (High solar ambient, direct daylight, anti-glare)
   -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
[data-theme="light"] {
  --canvas-bg: #F4F7FB;
  --canvas-subtle: #EAEFF6;
  --surface-card: #FFFFFF;
  --surface-elevated: #FFFFFF;
  --surface-hover: #F1F5F9;
  --surface-active: #E2E8F0;
  --surface-sunken: #E5ECF4;

  --text-bright: #09101D;

# `busverse/00-OVERVIEW.md`

**Document:** `busverse/00-OVERVIEW.md`
**Project:** BusVerse + BusCon
**Version:** 1.0
**Status:** Locked — Product Document
**Owner:** Shaghaf
**Guide:** Mr. Mohit Srivastav
**Purpose:** The map for the BusVerse passenger surface. Explains what BusVerse is, who uses it, what it does, what it doesn't do, and how the six BusVerse docs fit together. This doc is a navigation aid — it points to foundational docs and product detail docs rather than duplicating them. If you are new to BusVerse, start here.

> **Derives from:** `01-PLAN-AND-SCOPE.md` (scope), `02-REQUIREMENTS.md` §6.3 (48 FRs), `03-SYSTEM-ARCHITECTURE.md` §5 (container), `04-ACTORS-ROLES-PERMISSIONS.md` §3.2 (Passenger), `08-WORKFLOWS.md` §4 (7 workflows), `docs/assets/brand/busverse-icon-pack.html` (brand).
>
> **Referenced by:** `busverse/01-PASSENGER-WORKFLOWS.md`, `busverse/02-SEAT-MATRIX-UI.md`, `busverse/03-PAYMENT-REFUND-FLOW.md`, `busverse/04-TICKET-AND-TRACKING.md`, `busverse/05-UI-SPEC.md`, `report/14-MODULE_DESCRIPTION.md`, `report/15-SCREENSHOTS_UI.md`, `viva/03-DEMO_SCRIPT.md`.

## 2. What BusVerse Is

### 2.1 Positioning

**Diagram: BusVerse position**
*BusVerse is the passenger-facing frontend. It sits between passengers and the API. Behind the API are BusCon (operator + admin) and the Operator App (driver). Passengers only ever see BusVerse.*

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    PAX["Passenger"]
    BV["BusVerse<br/>web + PWA"]
    API["API"]
    BC["BusCon<br/>operator + admin"]
    OA["Operator App<br/>driver"]

    PAX --> BV
    BV --> API
    API --> BC
    API --> OA

    style BV fill:#0055ED,color:#fff
```

**Positioning statement:**

> BusVerse is the passenger-facing web application and Progressive Web App for booking intercity bus tickets in India. It is the demand side of a two-sided marketplace, connecting travelers with verified operators. BusVerse handles the full passenger journey — from search and seat selection through payment, ticketing, and live trip tracking.

### 2.2 What BusVerse is NOT

| Not | Why |
|---|---|
| A native mobile app | PWA only, for Phase 1 (Phase 2: native) |
| An operator panel | That's BusCon |
| A driver app | That's Operator App |
| A booking aggregator | Only lists operators on BusVerse |
| A payment processor | Uses Razorpay |
| A content site | Single-purpose product |

### 2.3 Where BusVerse sits in Phase 1

| Element | Value |
|---|---|
| Form factor | Next.js 15 web app + PWA |
| Deploy target | Vercel |
| Primary device | Mobile (360px+) |
| Secondary device | Desktop |
| Launch region | Delhi–Kanpur–Lucknow corridor |
| Users | Passenger (Guest + Registered) |

**Reference:** `01-PLAN-AND-SCOPE.md` §3 (surfaces), `03-SYSTEM-ARCHITECTURE.md` §5 (container).

---

## 3. Actor Context

**Diagram: BusVerse actors**
*Two passenger roles use BusVerse — Guest and Registered Passenger. No operator, driver, or admin uses BusVerse. Guests can browse; registered passengers get full functionality.*

```mermaid
flowchart TB
    BV["BusVerse"]

    G["Guest<br/>L0 trust<br/>browse only"]
    RP["Registered Passenger<br/>L1 trust<br/>full access"]

    G -->|"register"| RP
    G --> BV
    RP --> BV
```

### 3.1 Passenger roles

| Role | Trust level | Access |
|---|---|---|
| **Guest** | L0 (public) | Browse, search, view schedules, see seat availability |
| **Registered Passenger** | L1 (own) | Book, pay, view tickets, track, cancel, view history, profile |

### 3.2 What each role can do in BusVerse

| Action | Guest | Registered |
|---|:---:|:---:|
| Search | ✅ | ✅ |
| View seat availability | ✅ | ✅ |
| Lock a seat | ❌ | ✅ |
| Create booking | ❌ | ✅ |
| Pay | ❌ | ✅ |
| View ticket | ❌ | ✅ |
| Track trip | ❌ | ✅ |
| Cancel booking | ❌ | ✅ |
| View booking history | ❌ | ✅ |
| Update profile | ❌ | ✅ |

**Reference:** `04-ACTORS-ROLES-PERMISSIONS.md` §3.2 (Passenger identity), §9.1 (BusVerse permission grid).

---

## 4. Surface Boundary

**Diagram: BusVerse route groups**
*BusVerse is one Next.js app with six route groups. Each group covers one feature area. Groups map to product docs.*

```mermaid
flowchart TB
    BV["BusVerse<br/>Next.js 15"]

    BV --> R1["/(auth)<br/>login · OTP"]
    BV --> R2["/(search)<br/>home · search · results"]
    BV --> R3["/(booking)<br/>seat deck · form · checkout"]
    BV --> R4["/(tickets)<br/>my-trips · ticket"]
    BV --> R5["/(tracking)<br/>live tracking"]
    BV --> R6["/(profile)<br/>profile · cancel"]
```

### 4.1 Surface boundary rules

| Rule | Enforcement |
|---|---|
| Only Passenger and Guest can use BusVerse | Auth middleware in Next.js |
| No operator or admin features | No routes exist |
| No driver features | No routes exist |
| API-only communication with backend | No direct DB access from browser |
| WebSocket for real-time updates | Polling only as fallback |

### 4.2 What BusVerse owns in the system

| Owns | Where specified |
|---|---|
| Passenger UI | This doc, `05-UI-SPEC` |
| Passenger session management | `04` §8, `07` §3 |
| PWA offline cache for tickets | `04` §4 |
| Live tracking UI | `04` §4, `07` §8 |

### 4.3 What BusVerse does NOT own

| Not owned | Owned by |
|---|---|
| Backend business logic | API (`03` §6) |
| Seat lock atomicity | API + Redis (`03` §10) |
| Payment processing | Razorpay (`03` §12, `09` §13) |
| QR ticket signing | API (`09` §7) |
| Operator permissions | BusCon (`04` §9) |

---

## 6. Workflow Map

**Diagram: Passenger workflow flow**
*Seven passenger workflows from `08` §4. They flow linearly from register → search → lock → book + pay → ticket → track. Cancel is a side branch. Each workflow covers a set of FRs.*

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    W1["WF-P-01<br/>Register + login"] --> W2["WF-P-02<br/>Search and browse"]
    W2 --> W3["WF-P-03<br/>Seat selection + lock"]
    W3 --> W4["WF-P-04<br/>Book and pay"]
    W4 --> W5["WF-P-05<br/>View ticket"]
    W5 --> W6["WF-P-06<br/>Live tracking"]
    W4 --> W7["WF-P-07<br/>Cancel booking"]
```

### 6.1 Workflow → doc map

| Workflow | Covers FRs | Primary doc | Detail location |
|---|---|---|---|
| WF-P-01 Register + login | FR-BV-001 to FR-BV-004 | `01-PASSENGER-WORKFLOWS` | `08` §4.1 |
| WF-P-02 Search and browse | FR-BV-005 to FR-BV-010 | `01-PASSENGER-WORKFLOWS` | `08` §4.2 |
| WF-P-03 Seat selection + lock | FR-BV-011 to FR-BV-018 | `02-SEAT-MATRIX-UI` | `08` §4.3 |
| WF-P-04 Book and pay | FR-BV-019 to FR-BV-033 | `03-PAYMENT-REFUND-FLOW` | `08` §4.4 |
| WF-P-05 View ticket | FR-BV-034 to FR-BV-038 | `04-TICKET-AND-TRACKING` | `08` §4.5 |
| WF-P-06 Live tracking | FR-BV-039 to FR-BV-041 | `04-TICKET-AND-TRACKING` | `08` §4.6 |
| WF-P-07 Cancel booking | FR-BV-042 to FR-BV-045 | `03-PAYMENT-REFUND-FLOW` | `08` §4.7 |

### 6.2 Negative workflows affecting BusVerse

| Workflow | When triggered | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| WF-N-01 Payment fails → retry | Razorpay returns failure | `08` §9.1 |
| WF-N-02 Lock expires during checkout | 5 min TTL exceeded | `08` §9.2 |

**Reference:** `08-WORKFLOWS.md` §4 (7 workflows), §9 (negative).

## 7. Screen Catalog

**Diagram: Screen flow**
*The 11 screens in BusVerse and how users move between them. Solid arrows = primary flow. Dashed = secondary actions. Screen names come from `08` §3.5.*

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    HOME["SCR-BV-HOME"] --> SEARCH["SCR-BV-SEARCH"]
    SEARCH --> RESULTS["SCR-BV-RESULTS"]
    RESULTS --> SEATDECK["SCR-BV-SEATDECK"]
    SEATDECK --> PAXFORM["SCR-BV-PAXFORM"]
    PAXFORM --> CHECKOUT["SCR-BV-CHECKOUT"]
    CHECKOUT --> TICKET["SCR-BV-TICKET"]
    TICKET --> MYTRIPS["SCR-BV-MYTRIPS"]
    MYTRIPS --> TRACKING["SCR-BV-TRACKING"]
    MYTRIPS --> CANCEL["SCR-BV-CANCEL"]
    MYTRIPS --> PROFILE["SCR-BV-PROFILE"]

    MYTRIPS -.->|"resume"| SEATDECK
```

### 7.1 Screen catalog

| ID | Screen | Purpose | Primary workflow | Doc |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| SCR-BV-HOME | Home + login | Entry point, auth | WF-P-01 | `01-PASSENGER-WORKFLOWS` |
| SCR-BV-SEARCH | Search form | City + date input | WF-P-02 | `01-PASSENGER-WORKFLOWS` |
| SCR-BV-RESULTS | Results list | Schedule list with filters | WF-P-02 | `01-PASSENGER-WORKFLOWS` |
| SCR-BV-SEATDECK | 2D seat deck | Seat selection + lock | WF-P-03 | `02-SEAT-MATRIX-UI` |
| SCR-BV-PAXFORM | Passenger details | Per-passenger fields | WF-P-04 | `01-PASSENGER-WORKFLOWS` |
| SCR-BV-CHECKOUT | Razorpay checkout | Payment initiation | WF-P-04 | `03-PAYMENT-REFUND-FLOW` |
| SCR-BV-TICKET | QR ticket | Ticket display | WF-P-05 | `04-TICKET-AND-TRACKING` |
| SCR-BV-MYTRIPS | Trip list | Booking history | WF-P-05 | `01-PASSENGER-WORKFLOWS` |
| SCR-BV-TRACKING | Live map | Real-time bus tracking | WF-P-06 | `04-TICKET-AND-TRACKING` |
| SCR-BV-CANCEL | Cancel + refund preview | Cancel flow | WF-P-07 | `03-PAYMENT-REFUND-FLOW` |
| SCR-BV-PROFILE | Profile | User details | — | `01-PASSENGER-WORKFLOWS` |

### 7.2 Screen states

Each screen has multiple states to design for:

| Screen | States |
|---|---|
| SCR-BV-HOME | Logged out · logged in · OTP sent · OTP verified |
| SCR-BV-SEARCH | Empty · typed · autocomplete open · validation error |
| SCR-BV-RESULTS | Loading · results · empty · error |
| SCR-BV-SEATDECK | Loading · loaded · seat selected · lock acquired · lock expiring · lock expired |
| SCR-BV-PAXFORM | Empty · filled · validation error |
| SCR-BV-CHECKOUT | Razorpay loading · Razorpay open · payment success · payment failure |
| SCR-BV-TICKET | QR rendering · QR scanned · offline view |
| SCR-BV-MYTRIPS | Loading · list · empty · error |
| SCR-BV-TRACKING | Loading · map · connection lost · stale position |
| SCR-BV-CANCEL | Preview · confirmation · success · error |
| SCR-BV-PROFILE | Loaded · editing |

**Reference:** Detailed wireframes and states in `busverse/05-UI-SPEC.md`.

## 9. Journey from Search to Boarding

**Diagram: Passenger journey**
*The full passenger journey from opening the app to boarding the bus. This is the narrative summary of the 7 workflows. Maps directly to the 9 demo steps from `11` §10.*

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    J1["Open app<br/>search"] --> J2["See results<br/>pick bus"]
    J2 --> J3["Seat deck<br/>lock seat"]
    J3 --> J4["Fill form<br/>pay"]
    J4 --> J5["Get QR ticket<br/>in My Trips"]
    J5 --> J6["Watch bus<br/>on map"]
    J6 --> J7["Show QR<br/>at boarding"]
    J7 --> J8["Boarded<br/>trip ends"]
```

### 9.1 Journey step → workflow → demo mapping

| # | Step | Screen | Workflow | Demo step (`11` §10) |
|:---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Open app, search Delhi → Lucknow | SCR-BV-SEARCH | WF-P-02 | Demo 1 |
| 2 | See results, pick 14:00 departure | SCR-BV-RESULTS | WF-P-02 | Demo 1 |
| 3 | View seat deck, tap seat A1 | SCR-BV-SEATDECK | WF-P-03 | Demo 2 |
| 4 | Fill passenger form, tap Pay | SCR-BV-PAXFORM → CHECKOUT | WF-P-04 | Demo 3 |
| 5 | View QR ticket in My Trips | SCR-BV-TICKET | WF-P-05 | Demo 4 |
| 6 | Track bus on map | SCR-BV-TRACKING | WF-P-06 | Demo 7 |
| 7 | Show QR at boarding | (ticket display) | (driver-side WF-D-04) | Demo 8 |
| 8 | Passenger sees "Boarded" | SCR-BV-MYTRIPS | — | Demo 8 |

### 9.2 Journey timing targets

| Step | Target duration | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Search | ≤ 30 sec | Fast if user knows route |
| Results → seat lock | ≤ 60 sec | Includes comparison |
| Form + pay | ≤ 120 sec | Includes Razorpay checkout |
| Ticket view | ≤ 5 sec | Immediate after payment |
| Total booking flow | ≤ 5 min | End-to-end from search to ticket |

**Reference:** Full journey with narration in `11-DEPLOYMENT-AND-DEMO.md` §10.

## 10. Technical Stack

**Diagram: BusVerse stack**
*BusVerse is a Next.js 15 app. Consumes the shared API via HTTPS + WebSocket. Uses React Query for server state. PWA-capable. Deployed to Vercel.*

```mermaid
flowchart TB
    BV["BusVerse"]

    BV --> F["Framework<br/>Next.js 15 App Router"]
    BV --> UI["UI<br/>@busverse/ui<br/>@busverse/design-tokens"]
    BV --> ST["State<br/>React Query + Context"]
    BV --> R["Routing<br/>App Router + route groups"]
    BV --> PWA["PWA<br/>service worker<br/>offline cache"]
    BV --> DEP["Deploy<br/>Vercel"]
```

### 10.1 Stack details

| Layer | Tech | Reason (ADR) |
|---|---|---|
| Framework | Next.js 15 App Router | ADR-003 — SSR, PWA, Vercel |
| UI library | `@busverse/ui` + `@busverse/design-tokens` | Shared with BusCon |
| State | React Query + Context | `03` §7.1 — no Redux |
| Styling | Tailwind v4 | Reads design tokens |
| Icons | `@busverse/brand-svg` | Shared brand assets |
| PWA | Next.js built-in | Offline ticket view |
| Deploy | Vercel | ADR-013 |

### 10.2 Frontend architecture

| Concern | Implementation |
|---|---|
| Server state | React Query cache (5s staleTime) |
| Auth state | Context provider (JWT + user) |
| UI state | `useState` / `useReducer` (component-scoped) |
| WebSocket | Custom hook wrapping Socket.io client |
| PWA offline | Service worker + IndexedDB for ticket cache |
| API client | Typed fetch wrapper + React Query |

**Reference:** `03-SYSTEM-ARCHITECTURE.md` §5 (container), §7 (components), §21 (ADRs).

---

## 11. External Dependencies

**Diagram: External dependencies**
*BusVerse talks directly to the API and to OSM tiles. It opens Razorpay checkout directly (via redirect) but that's a one-time navigation, not a data dependency. All other external services are proxied via the API.*

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    BV["BusVerse"] -->|"HTTPS + WS"| API["API"]
    BV -->|"HTTP tiles"| OSM["OSM tiles"]
    BV -.->|"opens checkout"| RZP["Razorpay"]

    API --> RZP2["Razorpay"]
    API --> FB["Firebase"]
    API --> R2["R2"]
```

### 11.1 Dependency list

| Dependency | Purpose | Failure mode |
|---|---|---|
| API | All data + auth | Full outage |
| OSM tiles | Map rendering | Static fallback |
| Razorpay checkout | Payment UI | Error message |
| `@busverse/design-tokens` | Brand tokens | Build failure |

### 11.2 Fallback behavior

| Dependency | If unavailable |
|---|---|
| API | Show "service unavailable" — full outage |
| OSM tiles | Static fallback image with markers |
| Razorpay | Show "payments unavailable, try again" |
| WebSocket | Fall back to HTTP polling |

**Reference:** `02-REQUIREMENTS.md` §10.

## 12. Performance Targets

**Diagram: Performance targets**
*Six performance targets applied to BusVerse screens. Search is the most latency-sensitive (p95 < 200ms). Initial page load is 2s.*

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    T["BusVerse targets"]

    T --> T1["Search<br/>p95 < 200ms"]
    T --> T2["Seat deck load<br/>p95 < 300ms"]
    T --> T3["Book<br/>p95 < 500ms"]
    T --> T4["Pay init<br/>p95 < 800ms"]
    T --> T5["Track update<br/>every 5s"]
    T --> T6["Page load<br/>p95 < 2s"]
```

### 12.1 Target table

| Target | Value | Source | Test |
|---|---|---|---|
| Search response | p95 < 200 ms | `02` §8.1 | `perf_search` |
| Seat deck load | p95 < 300 ms | Estimated | `contract_availability` |
| Booking creation | p95 < 500 ms | `02` §8.1 | `perf_booking` |
| Payment initiation | p95 < 800 ms | `02` §8.1 | `contract_payments` |
| GPS position update | Every 5 sec | `07` §8.6 | `ws_security_*` |
| Initial page load (SSR) | p95 < 2 sec | Estimated | Lighthouse |

### 12.2 What this means in practice

| Scenario | Budget |
|---|---|
| User taps search | 200ms → results visible |
| User picks seat | 300ms → seat deck renders |
| User pays | 800ms → Razorpay checkout opens |
| User books | 500ms → booking confirmed state |

**Reference:** `02-REQUIREMENTS.md` §8.1.

## 17. Summary

**BusVerse is the passenger-facing web app and PWA for booking intercity bus tickets in India.** It is one of three codebases in the BusVerse + BusCon system, deployed on Vercel, built with Next.js 15, and serving the demand side of a two-sided marketplace. Only two actor roles use BusVerse — Guest (browse, search, view availability) and Registered Passenger (full access — book, pay, track, cancel, history, profile). Operators, drivers, and admins use BusCon or the Operator App instead; BusVerse has no routes for them. The surface spans six Next.js route groups: `/(auth)`, `/(search)`, `/(booking)`, `/(tickets)`, `/(tracking)`, `/(profile)`. It implements 48 functional requirements (37 Must-Have, 11 Should-Have) across nine feature areas — auth (4), search (6), seat selection (8), booking (8), payment (7), ticket (5), tracking (3), cancel (4), profile (3). These 48 FRs are orchestrated by seven workflows (WF-P-01 through WF-P-07) — register + login, search + browse, seat selection + lock, book + pay, view ticket, live tracking, cancel booking — each with its own primary detail doc. The 11 screens (SCR-BV-HOME through SCR-BV-PROFILE) each have multiple states to design for — loading, loaded, error, plus feature-specific states like lock countdown, seat selected, QR rendering, connection lost. Brand identity uses Royal Blue (`#0055ED` light, `#3B82F6` dark) with 11 design tokens shared across all BusVerse UI via `@busverse/design-tokens`. The passenger journey runs 8 steps — open app, search, seat deck, fill form + pay, get ticket, watch map, show QR at boarding, boarded — with a total booking flow target of ≤5 minutes and each step mapped to a workflow and to a demo step in `11-DEPLOYMENT-AND-DEMO.md` §10. Technical stack is Next.js 15 App Router, React Query for server state, Context for auth, Tailwind v4 for styling, `@busverse/ui` + `@busverse/design-tokens` + `@busverse/brand-svg` for shared components, PWA with service worker for offline ticket cache. BusVerse talks directly to the API (HTTPS REST + WebSocket) and OSM tiles (for map rendering); it opens Razorpay checkout directly but all payment state flows back through the API. Six performance targets apply — search p95 < 200ms, seat deck p95 < 300ms, booking p95 < 500ms, payment init p95 < 800ms, GPS update every 5s, initial page load p95 < 2s. Scope is strict — Phase 1 ships the full booking lifecycle (search, book, pay, ticket, track, cancel, PWA offline); Phase 2 adds reviews, coupons, waitlist, loyalty, native app, female-only filter, share-trip link, SOS button, Hindi, saved passengers; non-goals (owning buses, aggregator license, selling user data, raw card storage) are permanent. This overview is the entry point to a six-doc series — `00-OVERVIEW` (this), `01-PASSENGER-WORKFLOWS` (auth, search, booking, profile), `02-SEAT-MATRIX-UI` (seat deck), `03-PAYMENT-REFUND-FLOW` (Razorpay + cancellation), `04-TICKET-AND-TRACKING` (QR + live tracking), `05-UI-SPEC` (design system + wireframes). Recommended reading order for new contributors: 00 → 05 → 01 → 02 → 03 → 04. Ten open questions remain, all with Phase-2 defaults.**

---

**End of Document `busverse/00-OVERVIEW.md` v1.0.**

## 3. Auth Flow (WF-P-01)

### 3.1 Flow sequence

**Diagram: Auth flow sequence**
*Passenger enters phone → OTP sent via Firebase → user enters OTP → server verifies → access + refresh tokens issued → session stored → redirect to home. Six-digit OTP auto-advances. Paste fills all 6 digits. Resend timer 30 seconds.*

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    actor P as Passenger
    participant HOME as SCR-BV-HOME
    participant APP as App
    participant API as API
    participant FB as Firebase Auth

    P->>HOME: Enter phone
    HOME->>HOME: Validate E.164 format
    HOME->>APP: Submit phone
    APP->>API: POST /v1/passenger/auth/otp/request
    API->>FB: Send OTP
    API-->>APP: { expiresInSeconds: 600, attempts: 0 }
    APP-->>HOME: Show OTP input + 30s resend timer

    P->>HOME: Enter 6-digit OTP
    HOME->>APP: Auto-submit on 6th digit
    APP->>API: POST /v1/passenger/auth/otp/verify
    API->>FB: Verify
    API->>API: Issue JWT + refresh
    API-->>APP: { accessToken, refreshToken, user }
    APP->>APP: Store access in memory, refresh in cookie
    APP-->>HOME: Redirect to search
```

### 3.2 Auth state machine

**Diagram: Auth state machine**
*Auth flow has five states. Each state has defined transitions and error exits. User can back out at any point.*

```mermaid
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> LoggedOut
    LoggedOut --> PhoneEntered: enter phone
    PhoneEntered --> PhoneEntered: validation error
    PhoneEntered --> OTPWaiting: OTP requested
    OTPWaiting --> OTPEntered: enter code
    OTPEntered --> OTPWaiting: wrong OTP
    OTPEntered --> LoggedIn: verify success
    OTPWaiting --> PhoneEntered: resend / change phone
    LoggedIn --> LoggedOut: logout
    LoggedIn --> LoggedIn: session refresh
```

**State details:**

| State | UI | Available actions |
|---|---|---|
| LoggedOut | Phone input | Enter phone, browse search |
| PhoneEntered | Phone input + validation | Submit phone, edit phone |
| OTPWaiting | OTP input + resend button | Enter OTP, resend, back |
| OTPEntered | Loading state | — |
| LoggedIn | Home / search | Full access |

### 3.3 Screen — SCR-BV-HOME

**Screen purpose:** Entry point for BusVerse. Doubles as auth and home landing.

**Layout:**

| Region | Content (logged out) | Content (logged in) |
|---|---|---|
| **Top bar** | BusVerse logo | BusVerse logo · profile avatar |
| **Hero** | "Every bus. One platform." | "Search buses" |
| **Auth / Search** | Phone input + Continue button | Search form (from/from/to/date) |
| **Below** | Trust signals (verified operators, live tracking) | Popular routes (Phase 2) |
| **Bottom** | Login link | My trips link |

### 3.4 Auth components

**Diagram: Auth component structure**
*Home screen decomposed into components. AuthForm is the top-level auth component. It renders PhoneInput or OTPInput depending on state. Both share primitives from `@busverse/ui`.*

```mermaid
flowchart TB
    HOME["<HomeScreen>"]
    HOME --> HERO["<Hero>"]
    HOME --> AUTH["<AuthForm>"]

    AUTH --> PHONE["<PhoneInput><br/>state: logged_out"]
    AUTH --> OTP["<OTPInput><br/>state: otp_waiting"]
    AUTH --> SUCCESS["<RedirectToSearch><br/>state: logged_in"]

    OTP --> RESEND["<ResendTimer>"]
    OTP --> EDIT["<EditPhoneButton>"]

    PHONE --> UI["Shared primitives<br/><Button> · <Input> · <FormError>"]
    OTP --> UI
```

**Component contracts:**

| Component | Props | Emits |
|---|---|---|
| `<AuthForm>` | `initialPhone?` | `onLoginSuccess(user)` |
| `<PhoneInput>` | `value`, `onChange`, `onSubmit`, `isLoading`, `error` | `onSubmit(phone)` |
| `<OTPInput>` | `phone`, `onVerify`, `onResend`, `isLoading`, `error`, `resendSeconds` | `onVerify(otp)`, `onResend()` |
| `<ResendTimer>` | `seconds` | `onExpire` |

### 3.5 Auth API calls

| Endpoint | When | Request | Response |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /v1/passenger/auth/otp/request` | Phone submitted | `{ phone, intent }` | `{ expiresInSeconds: 600 }` |
| `POST /v1/passenger/auth/otp/verify` | OTP submitted | `{ phone, otp }` | `{ accessToken, refreshToken, user }` |
| `POST /v1/passenger/auth/refresh` | Access token expired | `{}` (cookie sent) | `{ accessToken, refreshToken }` |
| `POST /v1/passenger/auth/logout` | Logout clicked | `{}` | `204 No Content` |

**Reference:** Full contracts in `07-API-AND-REALTIME-CONTRACTS.md` §3.3.

### 3.6 Validation rules

**Phone:**

| Rule | Regex / check | Error message |
|---|---|---|
| E.164 format | `/^\+\d{10,15}$/` | "Enter a valid phone number" |
| Indian number | `/^\+91[6-9]\d{9}$/` | "Enter a valid Indian mobile" |
| Required | Not empty | "Phone number required" |

**OTP:**

| Rule | Check | Error message |
|---|---|---|
| Length | 6 digits | "OTP must be 6 digits" |
| Numeric | `/^\d{6}$/` | "OTP must be numeric" |
| Required | Not empty | "OTP required" |

### 3.7 Session storage

**Diagram: Session storage**
*Access token in memory (React state). Refresh token in httpOnly secure cookie. On page reload, silently refresh to get new access token. Logout clears both.*

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    LOGIN["Login success"] --> MEM["accessToken<br/>(React state)"]
    LOGIN --> COOKIE["refreshToken<br/>(httpOnly cookie)"]

    MEM --> REQ["API request<br/>Authorization: Bearer {token}"]
    COOKIE --> REFRESH["POST /auth/refresh"]

    RELOAD["Page reload"] --> REFRESH
    REFRESH --> MEM

    LOGOUT["Logout"] --> CLEAR["Clear memory + cookie"]
```

**Storage rules:**

| Token | Storage | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Access token | React memory (never localStorage) | XSS-resistant |
| Refresh token | httpOnly, Secure, SameSite=Lax cookie | XSS-resistant, CSRF-resistant |
| User object | React memory | Refetched on reload |

**Rule:** Never store JWT in `localStorage` or `sessionStorage`. Both are XSS-vulnerable.

### 3.8 Session refresh

**Diagram: Session refresh flow**
*On 401 response, client triggers silent refresh. If refresh succeeds, retries original request. If refresh fails, redirects to login with return URL. Single-flight lock prevents multiple simultaneous refreshes.*

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant APP as App
    participant API as API
    participant COOKIE as Refresh cookie
    participant LOGIN as Login

    APP->>API: GET /passenger/me
    API-->>APP: 401 Unauthorized

    APP->>APP: Acquire single-flight lock
    APP->>API: POST /auth/refresh (cookie)
    alt Refresh succeeds
        API-->>APP: New accessToken
        APP->>API: Retry GET /passenger/me
        API-->>APP: 200 OK
    else Refresh fails (401)
        API-->>APP: 401
        APP->>LOGIN: Redirect with return URL
    end
    APP->>APP: Release lock
```

**Refresh rules:**

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Trigger | Any 401 response |
| Single-flight | Only one refresh in flight at a time |
| Retry | Original request retried after refresh |
| Return URL | Save current URL, restore after login |
| Fail behavior | Clear session, redirect to home |

### 3.9 Error states

| Error | UI | Recovery |
|---|---|---|
| `INVALID_OTP` (400) | "Incorrect code. {attempts}/5" | Re-enter OTP |
| `OTP_EXPIRED` (400) | "Code expired. Request new code." | Tap resend |
| `OTP_ATTEMPTS_EXCEEDED` (429) | "Too many attempts. Try again in 15 min." | Wait |
| `RATE_LIMITED` (429) | "Please wait {seconds}s before retrying." | Wait |
| Network error | Toast: "Network unavailable. Retry?" | Tap retry |
| 500 | Toast: "Something went wrong. Retry." | Tap retry |

### 3.10 Edge cases

| Scenario | Behavior |
|---|---|
| User closes tab during OTP | Session restored on reopen (refresh token) |
| User switches device | Old sessions remain (multi-device allowed) |
| User logs in on second tab | New session issued; both tabs work |
| Session expires during form fill | Return URL preserved; redirected to login; session restored after |

---

## 4. Search Flow (WF-P-02)

### 4.1 Flow sequence

**Diagram: Search flow sequence**
*Passenger types origin → debounced autocomplete → selects from suggestions → destination → date picker → search. Results rendered on separate page with URL state. Filters and sort update URL.*

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    actor P as Passenger
    participant SEARCH as SCR-BV-SEARCH
    participant APP as App
    participant API as API
    participant RESULTS as SCR-BV-RESULTS

    P->>SEARCH: Type "Del"
    SEARCH->>SEARCH: Debounce 300ms
    SEARCH->>APP: Fetch cities
    APP->>API: GET /v1/public/cities?q=del
    API-->>APP: [{cityId, name, state}]
    APP-->>SEARCH: Show suggestions

    P->>SEARCH: Pick "Delhi (NCT)"
    SEARCH->>SEARCH: Set origin, focus destination

    P->>SEARCH: Type "Lucknow", pick
    SEARCH->>SEARCH: Set destination, focus date

    P->>SEARCH: Pick date
    SEARCH->>APP: Submit search
    APP->>RESULTS: Navigate with URL params
    RESULTS->>API: GET /v1/public/search?...
    API-->>RESULTS: [schedules]
    RESULTS-->>P: Rendered results
```

### 4.2 Search state flow

**Diagram: Search state flow**
*Search state is URL-first. Every filter, sort, or search change updates the URL. Browser back/forward works. Refresh preserves results.*

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    USER["User action"] --> STATE["State change"]
    STATE --> URL["URL updated"]
    URL --> FETCH["React Query fetch"]
    FETCH --> RESULTS["Results rendered"]

    BROWSER["Browser back"] --> URL
    SHARE["User shares URL"] --> FRIEND["Friend opens same URL"]
    FRIEND --> FETCH
```

**URL state structure:**

```
/busverse/search
  ?from=cty_delhi
  &to=cty_lucknow
  &date=2026-10-24
  &sort=departure_time
  &busType=AC_SEATER,SLEEPER
  &page=1
```

### 4.3 Screen — SCR-BV-SEARCH

**Layout:**

| Region | Content |
|---|---|
| **Top bar** | Logo · profile avatar (if logged in) |
| **Search card** | Origin · destination · date · Search button |
| **Recent searches** | Last 3 searches (localStorage) |
| **Popular routes** | Phase 2 |

**Search card components:**

| Component | Behavior |
|---|---|
| `<CityAutocomplete>` | Origin field, debounced search, keyboard nav |
| `<CityAutocomplete>` | Destination field, disabled if origin not set |
| `<DatePicker>` | Default today, min today, max +90 days |
| `<SearchButton>` | Disabled until all 3 filled |

### 4.4 Screen — SCR-BV-RESULTS

**Layout:**

| Region | Content |
|---|---|
| **Top bar** | Logo · profile avatar · edit search |
| **Filter bar** | Sort by time/price/duration · filters |
| **Results list** | Schedule cards |
| **Empty state** | "No buses found" + try different date |
| **Loading state** | Skeleton cards |

**Schedule card contents:**

| Row | Contents |
|---|---|
| 1 | Operator name · bus type badge |
| 2 | Departure time → Arrival time · duration |
| 3 | Origin stand · destination stand |
| 4 | Fare (from `{amountPaise}`) · Seats available |
| 5 | Select button |

### 4.5 Search components

**Diagram: Search component structure**
*Search and Results pages decomposed. Search page uses CityAutocomplete + DatePicker. Results page uses ScheduleCard, FilterBar, and sort controls.*

```mermaid
flowchart TB
    SEARCH["<SearchPage>"]
    RESULTS["<ResultsPage>"]

    SEARCH --> ORIGIN["<CityAutocomplete><br/>name=origin"]
    SEARCH --> DEST["<CityAutocomplete><br/>name=destination"]
    SEARCH --> DATE["<DatePicker>"]
    SEARCH --> BTN["<SearchButton>"]

    RESULTS --> BAR["<FilterBar>"]
    RESULTS --> SORT["<SortControl>"]
    RESULTS --> LIST["<ResultsList>"]
    LIST --> CARD["<ScheduleCard> × N"]

    BAR --> UI["Shared UI primitives"]
    SORT --> UI
    CARD --> UI
```

### 4.6 City autocomplete

**Diagram: Autocomplete behavior**
*Typing triggers debounced API call. Suggestions shown in dropdown. Keyboard navigable. Selection fills input and moves focus to next field.*

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    actor U as User
    participant INPUT as Input
    participant API as API
    participant LIST as Suggestions

    U->>INPUT: "D"
    INPUT->>INPUT: debounce 300ms (min 2 chars)
    U->>INPUT: "De"
    U->>INPUT: "Del"
    INPUT->>API: GET /cities?q=Del
    API-->>LIST: [suggestions]
    LIST-->>U: Show dropdown

    U->>LIST: Arrow down × 2
    U->>LIST: Enter
    LIST->>INPUT: "Delhi (NCT)"
    INPUT->>INPUT: Focus destination
```

**Autocomplete rules:**

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Trigger | After 300ms pause, min 2 chars |
| Abort | New keystroke aborts previous request |
| Cache | Same query cached for 60s |
| Keyboard | ↑↓ navigate, Enter selects, Esc closes |
| Mobile | Touch to select |
| Accessibility | ARIA combobox pattern |

### 4.7 Search API calls

| Endpoint | When | Request | Response |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /v1/public/cities?q=` | Autocomplete typing | Query param | Array of cities |
| `GET /v1/public/search` | Search submitted | Query params | Array of schedules |
| `GET /v1/public/schedules/:id` | Schedule clicked | Path param | Schedule detail |

**Reference:** Full contracts in `07-API-AND-REALTIME-CONTRACTS.md` §4.2.

### 4.8 Results handling

**Sort options:**

| Option | Default | Behavior |
|---|---|---|
| Departure time | ✅ | Earliest first |
| Price | ❌ | Cheapest first |
| Duration | ❌ | Shortest first |

**Filters (Phase 1):**

| Filter | Options |
|---|---|
| Bus type | AC Seater, AC Sleeper, Non-AC Seater |
| Amenities | Charging, WiFi, Water, Blanket |

**Empty state:**

When search returns 0 results:
- Message: "No buses found for this route on {date}"
- Actions: "Try next day", "Try different route"
- Show alternative dates with availability

**Loading state:**

- 3 skeleton cards with shimmers
- Filter bar shown (skeleton)
- Sort control shown (skeleton)

### 4.9 Search state management

**React Query keys:**

```
['cities', query]              // autocomplete
['search', { from, to, date, sort, filters }]  // search results
['schedule', scheduleId]       // individual schedule
```

**Cache rules:**

| Data | staleTime | gcTime |
|---|---|---|
| Cities list | 5 min | 10 min |
| Search results | 60 sec | 5 min |
| Schedule detail | 5 min | 10 min |

**Rule:** Search results cached by URL params. Back button returns cached results instantly, then refetches in background.

### 4.10 Search edge cases

| Scenario | Behavior |
|---|---|
| No cities match query | Show "No cities found. Try another spelling." |
| Same origin and destination | Error: "Origin and destination must be different" |
| Past date selected | Date picker blocks selection |
| No results | Empty state with suggestions |
| Network error | Toast: "Couldn't search. Retry?" |
| Search while logged out | Works — search is public |

## 5. Booking Form Flow (WF-P-04a)

### 5.1 Prerequisites

**This flow requires:**

| Prerequisite | Enforced by |
|---|---|
| Active seat lock from `02-SEAT-MATRIX-UI` | `lockId` in navigation state |
| User authenticated | Auth guard on route |
| Lock not expired | Checked on mount + submit |

**Rule:** If any prerequisite missing, redirect to seat deck with toast.

### 5.2 Flow sequence

**Diagram: Booking form sequence**
*Passenger lands on form with lock context. Fills passenger details. Form validates. Submits. Server verifies lock still active. Server creates PENDING booking. Redirect to payment.*

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    actor P as Passenger
    participant FORM as SCR-BV-PAXFORM
    participant APP as App
    participant API as API
    participant PAY as Payment (doc 03)

    P->>FORM: Navigate from seat deck
    FORM->>FORM: Read lockId from router state
    FORM->>API: GET /passenger/schedules/:id/availability
    API-->>FORM: Verify lock still valid

    P->>FORM: Fill passenger 1 (name, age, gender)
    P->>FORM: Fill passenger 2, ... N
    P->>FORM: Select boarding + dropping stands
    FORM->>FORM: Validate all fields

    P->>FORM: Tap Continue
    FORM->>API: POST /passenger/bookings
    API->>API: Verify lock expiry
    API->>API: Create PENDING booking
    API-->>FORM: { bookingId, status: PENDING }
    FORM->>PAY: Navigate to checkout
```

### 5.3 Screen — SCR-BV-PAXFORM

**Layout:**

| Region | Content |
|---|---|
| **Top bar** | Back to seat deck · lock countdown |
| **Route summary** | Delhi → Lucknow · 14:00 → 22:30 · 24 Oct |
| **Boarding stand** | Stand selector (from route stops) |
| **Dropping stand** | Stand selector (from route stops) |
| **Passenger 1** | Name, age, gender (required) |
| **Passenger 2..N** | Same, one card per seat |
| **Fare summary** | Per-seat fares + total |
| **Continue button** | Disabled until valid + lock active |

### 5.4 Multi-passenger design

**Diagram: Multi-passenger layout**
*One card per seat. Cards stacked vertically. Progress indicator shows which card is active. Each card independently validates.*

```mermaid
flowchart TB
    FORM["Passenger form"]

    FORM --> S1["Seat 1 card<br/>name · age · gender"]
    FORM --> S2["Seat 2 card<br/>name · age · gender"]
    FORM --> S3["Seat 3 card<br/>name · age · gender"]

    S1 --> V1["✓ valid"]
    S2 --> V2["⚠ invalid"]
    S3 --> V3["✗ empty"]

    V1 --> BTN["Continue<br/>disabled until all valid"]
    V2 --> BTN
    V3 --> BTN
```

**Multi-passenger rules:**

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Max passengers | 6 per booking |
| Min passengers | 1 |
| Each card | Independent fields |
| Progress | "Passenger 1 of 3" indicator |
| Active card | Auto-focus on load |
| Validation | Per-card, on blur |
| Same-as-first (Phase 2) | Not in Phase 1 |

### 5.5 Stand selection

**Boarding stand dropdown:**

| Source | Options |
|---|---|
| Route stands | From `08` WF-P-04 boarding stand selection |
| Default | Origin stand (first in route) |
| Constraint | Must be before dropping stand |

**Dropping stand dropdown:**

| Source | Options |
|---|---|
| Route stands | Same list |
| Default | Destination stand (last in route) |
| Constraint | Must be after boarding stand |

**Rule:** If user changes boarding to after dropping, dropping auto-advances to next stand.

### 5.6 Passenger fields

| Field | Type | Validation |
|---|---|---|
| Name | Text | 2–100 chars, no numbers |
| Age | Number | 1–120 |
| Gender | Select | MALE / FEMALE / OTHER |

**Field rules:**

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Required | All fields required |
| Trim | Leading/trailing spaces removed |
| Name | No special characters beyond space, hyphen, apostrophe |
| Age | Numeric only |
| Gender | Required select |

### 5.7 Lock countdown

**Diagram: Lock countdown states**
*Countdown visible at top of screen. Color changes based on time remaining. Submit blocked below 10 seconds.*

```mermaid
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> Normal: >60s remaining
    Normal --> Warning: 30-60s
    Warning --> Critical: 10-30s
    Critical --> Expired: 0s
    Expired --> [*]: redirect to seat deck
```

**Countdown behavior:**

| State | Color | Message | Submit |
|---|---|---|---|
| Normal | Neutral | "5:00 left" | Enabled |
| Warning | Yellow | "45s left" | Enabled |
| Critical | Red | "10s left" | Disabled |
| Expired | Red | "Lock expired" | Redirected |

### 5.8 Booking form API calls

| Endpoint | When | Request | Response |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /v1/passenger/schedules/:id/availability` | On mount | Path param | Availability + lock check |
| `POST /v1/passenger/bookings` | Submit | `{ lockId, boardingStandId, droppingStandId, passengers[] }` | `{ bookingId, status: PENDING }` |
| `GET /v1/passenger/bookings/:id` | On mount if resuming | Path param | Booking if exists |

**Reference:** Full contracts in `07-API-AND-REALTIME-CONTRACTS.md` §4.5.

### 5.9 Handoff to payment

**Diagram: Handoff to payment doc**
*Booking form completes → creates PENDING booking → navigates to checkout. Payment flow (Razorpay integration) is owned by `03`. This doc defines the entry point only.*

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    FORM["SCR-BV-PAXFORM<br/>(this doc)"] -->|"POST /bookings"| API["API"]
    API -->|"bookingId, PENDING"| NAV["Router state"]
    NAV --> CHECKOUT["SCR-BV-CHECKOUT<br/>(doc 03)"]

    CHECKOUT -.->|"payment success"| TICKET["SCR-BV-TICKET<br/>(doc 04)"]
```

**Router state contract:**

```typescript
{
  bookingId: string,
  scheduleId: string,
  lockId: string,        // for potential release
  totalFarePaise: number
}
```

**Rule:** If `bookingId` not in router state, checkout page redirects to home. Prevents deep-linking to checkout without a booking.

### 5.10 Booking form edge cases

| Scenario | Behavior |
|---|---|
| Lock expires while filling | Banner appears, submit disabled, redirect after 30s |
| Lock expires on submit | `400 LOCK_EXPIRED` → toast → redirect to seat deck |
| Network fails on submit | Retry button, form state preserved |
| User navigates back | Form state preserved in memory (not localStorage) |
| User refreshes page | Lock ID from URL, form reset, warning shown |
| Duplicate submit | Button disabled during submit |

## 6. Profile Flow

### 6.1 Flow sequence

**Diagram: Profile flow**
*Passenger views profile → optionally edits name or email → saves. Phone is immutable. Logout clears session.*

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    actor P as Passenger
    participant PROF as SCR-BV-PROFILE
    participant APP as App
    participant API as API

    P->>PROF: Open profile
    PROF->>API: GET /passenger/me
    API-->>PROF: { user, profile }

    P->>PROF: Edit name
    PROF->>PROF: Validate name
    P->>PROF: Save
    PROF->>API: PATCH /passenger/me { name }
    API-->>PROF: { updated }
    PROF-->>P: Toast "Saved"

    P->>PROF: Logout
    PROF->>API: POST /auth/logout
    API-->>PROF: 204
    PROF->>APP: Clear session
    APP-->>P: Redirect to home
```

### 6.2 Screen — SCR-BV-PROFILE

**Layout:**

| Region | Content |
|---|---|
| **Top bar** | Back · Edit toggle |
| **Avatar** | Initials or placeholder |
| **Name** | Editable input |
| **Phone** | Read-only with lock icon |
| **Email** | Editable input |
| **My trips link** | Navigate to My trips |
| **Support link** | Email link to support |
| **Logout button** | Destructive style |
| **App version** | Footer |

### 6.3 Profile API calls

| Endpoint | When | Request | Response |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /v1/passenger/me` | On mount | — | `{ user, profile }` |
| `PATCH /v1/passenger/me` | Save | `{ name?, email? }` | `{ user }` |
| `POST /v1/passenger/auth/logout` | Logout | — | `204` |

### 6.4 Profile field rules

| Field | Editable | Validation |
|---|---|---|
| Name | ✅ | 2–100 chars |
| Email | ✅ | Valid email, lowercased |
| Phone | ❌ | Immutable — locked at signup |

**Phone immutability:**

- Input has lock icon
- Tooltip: "Phone number can't be changed. Contact support to update."
- If user really needs to change → contact support (Phase 2: self-service)

### 6.5 Logout flow

**Diagram: Logout sequence**
*Logout clears both memory and cookie. Redirects to home. Back button doesn't restore session.*

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    actor U as User
    participant PROF as ProfilePage
    participant APP as App
    participant API as API

    U->>PROF: Tap logout
    PROF->>PROF: Confirm dialog
    U->>PROF: Confirm
    PROF->>API: POST /auth/logout
    API-->>PROF: 204
    PROF->>APP: Clear session (memory + cookie)
    APP->>APP: Clear React Query cache
    APP-->>U: Redirect to home (logged out)
```

**Logout rules:**

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Confirm dialog | "Log out? You'll need to log in again." |
| Server call | Fire and forget (best effort) |
| Client cleanup | Clear memory + cookie + React Query cache |
| Redirect | Home page, logged out state |
| Back button | Does not restore session |

## 8. State Management

### 8.1 State layering

**Diagram: State layering**
*Three layers of state. Server state in React Query. Auth state in Context. UI state in local hooks. Each layer has a defined owner and lifecycle.*

```mermaid
flowchart TB
    SERVER["Server state<br/>React Query"]
    AUTH["Auth state<br/>SessionProvider Context"]
    UI["UI state<br/>useState · useReducer"]

    SERVER --> COMP["Component"]
    AUTH --> COMP
    UI --> COMP

    SERVER --> CACHE["queryKey cache"]
    SERVER --> MUT["mutations"]
    AUTH --> TOK["token memory"]
    AUTH --> USER["user context"]
    UI --> FORM["form state"]
    UI --> MODAL["modal state"]
    UI --> LOAD["loading flags"]
```

### 8.2 React Query key structure

| Query | Key | staleTime |
|---|---|---|
| Current user | `['user']` | 5 min |
| Cities | `['cities', query]` | 5 min |
| Search results | `['search', { from, to, date, sort, filters }]` | 60 sec |
| Schedule detail | `['schedule', scheduleId]` | 5 min |
| Booking by ID | `['booking', bookingId]` | 30 sec |

### 8.3 Mutation → invalidation rules

| Mutation | Invalidates |
|---|---|
| Login | `['user']` |
| Logout | All queries (clear cache) |
| Create booking | `['booking', bookingId]`, `['bookings']` |
| Update profile | `['user']` |

### 8.4 Form state

**Library:** React Hook Form + Zod.

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Validation | Zod schema per form |
| Validation trigger | On blur + on submit |
| Error display | Field-level + form-level |
| Dirty state | Block navigation if unsaved changes |
| Reset | Clear on submit success |

---

## 9. Error Handling

### 9.1 Error classification

**Diagram: Error handling flow**
*Every error is classified then routed to the right UI response. Network errors are retryable. Auth errors redirect. Validation errors highlight fields. Server errors show generic message.*

```mermaid
flowchart TB
    ERR["Error caught"] --> CLASSIFY["Classify error"]

    CLASSIFY --> NET["Network error"]
    CLASSIFY --> AUTHERR["401 Unauthorized"]
    CLASSIFY --> FORBID["403 Forbidden"]
    CLASSIFY --> NF["404 Not found"]
    CLASSIFY --> CONF["409 Conflict"]
    CLASSIFY --> VALID["422 Validation"]
    CLASSIFY --> SERVER["5xx Server"]
    CLASSIFY --> RATE["429 Rate limited"]

    NET --> R1["Toast + retry button"]
    AUTHERR --> R2["Silent refresh → retry<br/>or redirect to login"]
    FORBID --> R3["Toast: not authorized"]
    NF --> R4["Empty state"]
    CONF --> R5["Specific message<br/>(e.g. seat taken)"]
    VALID --> R6["Field-level errors"]
    SERVER --> R7["Generic toast + Sentry"]
    RATE --> R8["Toast: retry in Xs"]
```

### 9.2 Error message rules

| Rule | Example |
|---|---|
| User-friendly | "Payments unavailable, please try again" |
| Actionable | Include next step |
| No stack traces | Never show internal details |
| Stable codes | `error.code` for logging |
| Sentry logged | Every 5xx + auth failures |

**Rule:** Full error code catalog in `07-API-AND-REALTIME-CONTRACTS.md` §9. Client maps codes to human messages in `errors.ts`.

### 9.3 Redirect rules

**Diagram: Redirect decision tree**
*When to redirect vs. show error inline. Auth issues redirect. Validation issues inline. Network issues retry.*

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    ERR["Error"]
    ERR --> Q1{"401?"}
    Q1 -->|Yes| Q2{"Refresh<br/>succeeds?"}
    Q2 -->|Yes| RETRY["Retry request"]
    Q2 -->|No| LOGIN["Redirect to login<br/>+ return URL"]

    Q1 -->|No| Q3{"422?"}
    Q3 -->|Yes| FORM["Inline field errors"]

    Q3 -->|No| Q4{"5xx?"}
    Q4 -->|Yes| TOAST["Toast + log"]

    Q4 -->|No| Q5{"Network?"}
    Q5 -->|Yes| RETRY2["Retry button"]
    Q5 -->|No| SHOW["Show error message"]
```

### 9.4 Error logging

| Error type | Logged to | Contains |
|---|---|---|
| 4xx (validation) | Not logged | — |
| 4xx (auth failure) | Server only | `requestId`, `userId` |
| 5xx | Sentry | Full context |
| Network error | Not logged | — |
| Rate limited | Server only | `requestId`, `userId` |

**Rule:** Never log PII. Sentry filters PII before shipping. `09` §11.5 defines scrubber.

---

## 10. Accessibility

### 10.1 Phase 1 requirements

| Requirement | Implementation |
|---|---|
| Keyboard navigation | All interactive elements reachable via Tab |
| Focus visible | Visible focus ring on all elements |
| Focus trap | Modals trap focus until closed |
| Focus return | Focus returns to trigger on modal close |
| Screen reader labels | Every input has `<label>` or `aria-label` |
| ARIA live | Toast announcements via `role="alert"` |
| Color contrast | ≥ 4.5:1 for text |
| Touch targets | ≥ 44×44px |
| Reduced motion | Respect `prefers-reduced-motion` |
| Form errors | `aria-invalid` on failed fields + `aria-describedby` |

### 10.2 Phase 2 deferrals

| Item | Phase 2 |
|---|---|
| Full WCAG 2.1 AA compliance | Phase 2 |
| Screen reader testing (VoiceOver, NVDA) | Phase 2 |
| High contrast mode | Phase 2 |
| Font size toggle | Phase 2 |

### 10.3 Accessibility patterns per component

| Component | Accessibility |
|---|---|
| `<Button>` | `aria-pressed` for toggles, `aria-disabled` when disabled |
| `<Input>` | Associated `<label>`, `aria-invalid`, `aria-describedby` |
| `<CityAutocomplete>` | `role="combobox"`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-activedescendant` |
| `<Modal>` | `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, focus trap |
| `<Toast>` | `role="alert"`, `aria-live="polite"` |
| `<LockCountdown>` | `role="timer"`, `aria-live="polite"` |
